# Codex conversation 01a0e38d-3f8b-7ca3-a4fc-50da5ec41528

## assistant

By “old exam copy,” I meant answers from an unfinished PrepTest saved in a browser before account saving was added. You do not need to make a decision about those now. We’ll preserve them and won’t guess which student owns them.

**Admissions is locked on the live site.** I verified that a signed-in student sees a lock in the rail and gets “Not open yet” when opening the direct URL. Joshua and admins retain access. The change passed 44 focused tests, TypeScript checking, and a production build.

Your other decisions are recorded in the [Grill Me notes](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md): a failed exam save pauses the clock and shows the problem, and the implementation spec does not need to wait for a student prototype test. The next step is to turn those settled decisions into the student-first implementation spec.

**Next is the Progress decision.** I checked the app: student Analytics, History, and teacher Insights currently count answers differently, so we need one rule before combining them on a Progress page.

My recommendation is to use a student’s **first graded answer** for diagnostic accuracy, show repeats separately in History and practice volume, label which sources each number includes, and use the already approved 7/14/21/30-day/All filter throughout. I’ve recorded that proposal in [decision #161](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/161).

Then I’ll settle Joshua’s read-only student preview, write the implementation spec, and split it into student-first build tickets. You don’t need to revisit the old exam-record question to keep this moving.

## My request:
[$wait-what](C:\Users\Joshu\\.agents\skills\wait-what\SKILL.md) what is student prevuew abotu andwhat is read only view mean 


## assistant

**Student preview** is a proposed button on a student’s page in your Teacher Workspace. For example, you would open Lydia’s page, choose **View student experience**, and see how her Home, My Work, Review, and Progress appear to her. It helps you check what she can find and what work needs attention. This feature has **not been built yet**.

**Read-only** means you can look, but you cannot act *as Lydia*. You cannot answer a question, mark her work complete, change her flags, or send a message from her account. A clear banner would say you are previewing Lydia’s view. To assign work or add a note, you return to your normal Teacher Workspace and act as yourself.

## assistant

Understood. I corrected the plan: when you view a student’s experience, **you keep full teacher and admin controls**. Changes you make to that student’s work are recorded as your actions.

You also chose that answers you give to questions stay separate. They will not change the student’s score, History, completion, or Review. I recorded this in [teacher scope decision #162](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/162). This is the rule for the redesign; the Teacher mode has not been built yet.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

Behavior (active state, click handlers) stays in\r\n// ClassroomLayout, keyed by id — this file owns only what the rail contains,\r\n// so the Account layout editor and the rail render from one source of truth.\r\n\r\nexport type RailGroupId = "classroom" | "study" | "review" | "support";\r\n\r\nexport type RailItemId =\r\n  | "home"\r\n  | "notes"\r\n  | "work"\r\n  | "drill"\r\n  | "testing"\r\n  | "bootcamps"\r\n  | "activities"\r\n  | "journal"\r\n  | "flagged"\r\n  | "analytics"\r\n  | "history"\r\n  | "admissions"\r\n  | "messages"\r\n  | "schedule";\r\n\r\nexport interface RailItem {\r\n  id: RailItemId;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  group: RailGroupId;\r\n  /** Empty = available to every signed-in student. */\r\n  flags: PermissionFlag[];\r\n  /**\r\n   * Mirrors the `allowSignedIn` prop on this item\'s route. Where the route lets\r\n   * any signed-in user through regardless of `flags`, the rail must not draw a\r\n   * padlock from those same flags — a padlock over a route anyone can open is\r\n   * a lie the student can disprove by typing the URL.\r\n   *\r\n   * Keep this in step with `src/App.tsx`. `railCatalog.test.ts` pins the pairs.\r\n   */\r\n  allowSignedIn?: boolean;\r\n  /** Mirrors `requireAdmin` on routes reserved for Joshua and admins. */\r\n  requireAdmin?: boolean;\r\n  /** Section items open the side list and render a › chevron when unlocked. */\r\n  section?: boolean;\r\n  /** Home can never be hidden — it is the guaranteed way back. */\r\n  required?: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport const RAIL_GROUPS: { id: RailGroupId; title: string }[] = [\r\n  { id: "classroom", title: "Classroom" },\r\n  { id: "study", title: "Study" },\r\n  { id: "review", title: "Review" },\r\n  { id: "support", title: "Support" },\r\n];\r\n\r\nexport const RAIL_CATALOG: RailItem[] = [\r\n  { id: "home", label: "Home", group: "classroom", flags: ["has_classroom_access"], allowSignedIn: true, required: true },\r\n  { id: "drill", label: "Drill", group: "classroom", flags: ["has_drill_access"] },\r\n  // The Testing Centre is where a student sits something under test conditions:\r\n  // one real timed section, or a full practice test. It is deliberately its own\r\n  // rail entry rather than a tab inside Drill — drilling is practice you stop\r\n  // and think inside, and a sitting is not, so putting them behind one door\r\n  // invites starting a 35:00 clock by accident.\r\n  { id: "testing", label: "Testing Centre", group: "classroom", flags: ["has_drill_access"] },\r\n  { id: "work", label: "Assigned work", group: "classroom", flags: ["has_classroom_access"], section: true },\r\n  { id: "notes", label: "Notes and recaps", group: "study", flags: ["has_classroom_access"], section: true },\r\n  { id: "bootcamps", label: "Bootcamps", group: "study", flags: ["has_bootcamp_access"] },\r\n  { id: "activities", label: "Activities", group: "study", flags: ["has_activity_access"] },\r\n  { id: "journal", label: "Wrong answers", group: "review", flags: ["has_journal_access", "has_waj_access"], section: true },\r\n  { id: "flagged", label: "Flagged", group: "review", flags: ["has_flagged_access"], section: true },\r\n  { id: "analytics", label: "Analytics", group: "review", flags: ["has_analytics_access"] },\r\n  { id: "admissions", label: "Admissions", group: "review", flags: [], requireAdmin: true },\r\n  { id: "history", label: "History", group: "review", flags: ["has_analytics_access"] },\r\n  { id: "messages", label: "Messages", group: "support", flags: ["has_chat_access"] },\r\n  // Schedule owns booking too: one rail entry, two stage tabs (Schedule | Book\r\n  // a session). Two adjacent Support entries for the same errand opened\r\n  // unrelated-looking screens, and the empty Schedule had no way to book at all.\r\n  { id: "schedule", label: "Schedule", group: "support", flags: ["has_schedule_access"], allowSignedIn: true },\r\n];\r\n\r\nexport interface RailPrefs {\r\n  /** Item ids in display order per group. Missing ids append in catalog order; unknown ids are dropped. */\r\n  order: Partial<Record<RailGroupId, string[]>>;\r\n  /** Item ids the student chose to hide. Required items are never hidden. */\r\n  hidden: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function defaultRailPrefs(): RailPrefs {\r\n  return { order: {}, hidden: [] };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function normalizeRailPrefs(raw: unknown): RailPrefs {\r\n  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return defaultRailPrefs();\r\n  const r = raw as Partial<RailPrefs>;\r\n  const validIds = new Set(RAIL_CATALOG.map((i) => i.id));\r\n  const order: RailPrefs["order"] = {};\r\n  if (r.order && typeof r.order === "object") {\r\n    for (const group of RAIL_GROUPS) {\r\n      const list = (r.order as Record<string, unknown>)[group.id];\r\n      if (Array.isArray(list)) {\r\n        order[group.id] = list.filter(\r\n          (id): id is string => typeof id === "string" && validIds.has(id as RailItemId),\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  const hidden = Array.isArray(r.hidden)\r\n    ? r.hidden.filter((id): id is string => {\r\n        if (typeof id !== "string" || !validIds.has(id as RailItemId)) return false;\r\n        const item = RAIL_CATALOG.find((i) => i.id === id);\r\n        return !item?.required;\r\n      })\r\n    : [];\r\n  return { order, hidden };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Resolve prefs into the per-group item lists the rail renders.\r\n * includeHidden keeps hidden items in place (the layout editor needs them).\r\n */\r\nexport function resolveRailGroups(\r\n  prefs: RailPrefs,\r\n  opts?: { includeHidden?: boolean },\r\n): {\r\n  id: RailGroupId;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  items: RailItem[];\r\n}[] {\r\n  const hidden = new Set(opts?.includeHidden ? [] : prefs.hidden);\r\n  return RAIL_GROUPS.map((group) => {\r\n    const catalogItems = RAIL_CATALOG.filter((i) => i.group === group.id);\r\n    const byId = new Map(catalogItems.map((i) => [i.id as string, i]));\r\n    const ordered: RailItem[] = [];\r\n    for (const id of prefs.order[group.id] ?? []) {\r\n      const item = byId.get(id);\r\n      if (item && !ordered.includes(item)) ordered.push(item);\r\n    }\r\n    for (const item of catalogItems) {\r\n      if (!ordered.includes(item)) ordered.push(item);\r\n    }\r\n    return {\r\n      ...group,\r\n      items: ordered.filter((i) => i.required || !hidden.has(i.id)),\r\n    };\r\n  }).filter((group) => group.items.length > 0);\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\nimport { useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport {\r\n  BarChart3,\r\n  LayoutGrid,\r\n  Library,\r\n  MessageSquare,\r\n  PanelRightClose,\r\n  PanelRightOpen,\r\n  PencilRuler,\r\n  type LucideIcon,\r\n} from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\nimport { OperatorChatProvider } from \'@/components/teacher/ai-ta/OperatorChatProvider\';\r\n\r\ntype Room = {\r\n  to: string;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  Icon: LucideIcon;\r\n  /** NavLink `end`: only Today, so exactly one item is ever active. */\r\n  end?: boolean;\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The four rooms in the teacher workspace. Students live in the classroom\r\n * side rail because roster access is a teacher-level destination, not a room\r\n * beside the teacher\'s daily work. This list is the only top navigation in\r\n * the teacher workspace: no `?ws=` switching, no second rail,\r\n * no per-surface tab bars pretending to be nav.\r\n *\r\n * `end` is set on Today alone. Every other room owns a path prefix, so its\r\n * child routes (library tabs, a student dossier, a thread) keep the parent\r\n * highlighted, and no two items can be active at once.\r\n */\r\nconst ROOMS: Room[] = [\r\n  { to: \'/teacher/today\', label: \'Today\', Icon: LayoutGrid, end: true },\r\n  { to: \'/teacher/inbox\', label: \'Inbox\', Icon: MessageSquare },\r\n  { to: \'/teacher/library\', label: \'Library\', Icon: Library },\r\n  { to: \'/teacher/insights\', label: \'Insights\', Icon: BarChart3 },\r\n];\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The perspective floor from the Desk. Kept so the workspace ground reads as\r\n * the same room the Desk surfaces were designed for.\r\n */\r\nconst FLOOR_GRID_STYLE: React.CSSProperties = {\r\n  transform: \'perspective(440px) rotateX(62deg)\',\r\n  transformOrigin: \'top center\',\r\n  backgroundImage:\r\n    \'linear-gradient(rgba(13,19,22,0.14) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(13,19,22,0.10) 1px, transparent 1px)\',\r\n  backgroundSize: \'46px 46px\',\r\n  maskImage: \'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)\',\r\n  WebkitMaskImage: \'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The teacher workspace shell: one top rail that never unmounts, one header,\r\n * one accent. Every room renders into the `<Outlet/>`; nothing here remounts\r\n * when the room changes, so room-to-room navigation is real browser history.\r\n *\r\n * The rail sits in the header rather than down the side: the shell is entered\r\n * from the Classroom\'s own side rail, and a second vertical rail immediately\r\n * inside the first read as two competing navigations. Rooms across the top,\r\n * each room\'s own tabs (Library\'s bootcamps/drills/assignments/activities) in\r\n * the workspace below — one level of nav per axis.\r\n *\r\n * The AI panel (spec D6) is a Phase 3 build. Its slot and toggle exist now so\r\n * the layout is settled before the panel lands in it.\r\n */\r\nexport default function TeacherWorkspaceShell() {\r\n  return (\r\n    <OperatorChatProvider>\r\n      <WorkspaceLayout />\r\n    </OperatorChatProvider>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction WorkspaceLayout() {\r\n  const location = useLocation();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const [aiPanelOpen, setAiPanelOpen] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    // Fills the classroom stage it is mounted into rather than claiming the\r\n    // viewport: the classroom rail sits beside it and owns the page height.\r\n    <div className="relative flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#c3cace] text-[#11181c]">\r\n      <div aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[56%] overflow-hidden">\r\n        <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 h-[220%] w-[320%] -translate-x-1/2" style={FLOOR_GRID_STYLE} />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">\r\n        <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 sm:px-6">\r\n          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-3.5">\r\n            <h1 className="truncate font-display text-[22px] leading-none tracking-tight">\r\n              Teacher Workspace\r\n            </h1>\r\n\r\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\r\n            <button\r\n              type="button"\r\n              onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/whiteboard\')}\r\n              className={cn(\r\n                \'inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 text-[12px] font-medium transition\',\r\n                location.pathname.startsWith(\'/teacher/whiteboard\')\r\n                  ? \'border-black bg-black text-white\'\r\n                  : \'border-black/15 text-black/60 hover:border-black/40 hover:text-black\',\r\n              )}\r\n            >\r\n              <PencilRuler className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\r\n              Whiteboard\r\n            </button>\r\n\r\n            <button\r\n              type="button"\r\n              onClick={() => setAiPanelOpen((open) => !open)}\r\n              aria-expanded={aiPanelOpen}\r\n              aria-controls="teacher-ai-panel"\r\n              className={cn(\r\n                \'inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 text-[12px] font-medium transition\',\r\n                aiPanelOpen\r\n                  ? \'border-teacher-accent bg-teacher-accent text-teacher-accent-foreground hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover\'\r\n                  : \'border-teacher-accent/40 text-teacher-accent-strong hover:bg-teacher-accent/10\',\r\n              )}\r\n            >\r\n              {aiPanelOpen ? (\r\n                <PanelRightClose className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\r\n              ) : (\r\n                <PanelRightOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\r\n              )}\r\n              Assistant\r\n            </button>\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          {/* The room rail. Hidden below md, where the bottom bar takes over. */}\r\n          <nav\r\n            className="hidden items-stretch gap-1 overflow-x-auto md:flex"\r\n            aria-label="Teacher workspace"\r\n          >\r\n            {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (\r\n              <NavLink\r\n                key={to}\r\n                to={to}\r\n                end={end}\r\n                className={({ isActive }) =>\r\n                  cn(\r\n                    // Deliberately heavier than a room\'s own tab row below it,\r\n                    // so two stacked horizontal strips can\'t read as the same\r\n                    // level of navigation.\r\n                    \'inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b-[3px] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition\',\r\n                    isActive\r\n                      ? \'border-teacher-accent font-semibold text-black\'\r\n                      : \'border-transparent text-black/50 hover:text-black\',\r\n                  )\r\n                }\r\n              >\r\n                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n                <span className="truncate">{label}</span>\r\n              </NavLink>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </nav>\r\n        </header>\r\n\r\n        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">\r\n          <main className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto">\r\n            <Outlet />\r\n          </main>\r\n\r\n          {aiPanelOpen ? (\r\n            <aside\r\n              id="teacher-ai-panel"\r\n              className="hidden w-[320px] shrink-0 border-l border-black/10 bg-white/40 md:flex md:flex-col"\r\n              aria-label="Assistant"\r\n            >\r\n              <div className="px-5 pb-3 pt-5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40">\r\n                Assistant\r\n              </div>\r\n              <p className="px-5 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-black/55">\r\n                The assistant moves in here, with whatever room you are in as its context. Nothing to use yet.\r\n              </p>\r\n            </aside>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {/* Mobile: the sidebar becomes a bottom bar. Admin has to work on a phone. */}\r\n        <nav\r\n          className="flex shrink-0 items-stretch overflow-x-auto border-t border-black/10 bg-[#c3cace] pb-[max(0.25rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] md:hidden"\r\n          aria-label="Teacher workspace"\r\n        >\r\n          {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (\r\n            <NavLink\r\n              key={to}\r\n              to={to}\r\n              end={end}\r\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\r\n                cn(\r\n                  \'flex min-w-[4.5rem] shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-0.5 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors\',\r\n                  isActive ? \'text-black\' : \'text-black/45\',\r\n                )\r\n              }\r\n            >\r\n              <Icon className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n              <span className="max-w-full truncate px-1">{label}</span>\r\n            </NavLink>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </nav>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q3\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate, useOutletContext, useParams } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport { ArrowLeft, Activity, Plus } from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { format } from \'date-fns\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { useSelectedHubStudent, useStudentEmail, type HubStudentRow } from \'@/hooks/useHubStudents\';\r\nimport { formatLastActiveLabel, isUserOnline } from \'@/lib/presence\';\r\nimport AssignmentBuilder from \'@/components/teacher/AssignmentBuilder\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The student dossier (spec T3.2): everything about one student behind one\r\n * URL, with the five surfaces as real child routes so each is linkable and\r\n * back/forward work. Replaces the split between TeacherStudentDetail, the\r\n * StudentHub page, and the roster\'s inline expander.\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport interface DossierContext {\r\n  studentId: string;\r\n  student: HubStudentRow | undefined;\r\n  studentName: string | null;\r\n  studentEmail: string | null;\r\n  /** Opens the shared assign flow already scoped to this student. */\r\n  openAssign: () => void;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useDossier() {\r\n  return useOutletContext<DossierContext>();\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst TABS = [\r\n  { to: \'overview\', label: \'Overview\' },\r\n  { to: \'work\', label: \'Work\' },\r\n  { to: \'notes\', label: \'Notes\' },\r\n  { to: \'messages\', label: \'Messages\' },\r\n  { to: \'schedule\', label: \'Schedule\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport default function StudentDossier() {\r\n  const { studentId } = useParams<{ studentId: string }>();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n\r\n  const student = useSelectedHubStudent(studentId ?? null);\r\n  // The roster query may not have resolved on a deep link, so fall back to the\r\n  // narrow per-student RPC rather than showing a nameless header.\r\n  const { data: rpcEmail } = useStudentEmail(studentId ?? null);\r\n\r\n  const [showAssign, setShowAssign] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const studentName = student?.display_name?.trim() || null;\r\n  const studentEmail = student?.email ?? rpcEmail ?? null;\r\n\r\n  const isOnline = isUserOnline(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveAbsolute = student?.last_seen_at\r\n    ? format(new Date(student.last_seen_at), \'MMM d, h:mm a\')\r\n    : null;\r\n\r\n  const context = useMemo<DossierContext>(\r\n    () => ({\r\n      studentId: studentId as string,\r\n      student,\r\n      studentName,\r\n      studentEmail,\r\n      openAssign: () => setShowAssign(true),\r\n    }),\r\n    [student, studentEmail, studentId, studentName],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  if (!studentId) return null;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="flex h-full w-full flex-col" aria-label="Student dossier">\r\n      <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 pb-0 pt-4">\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/students\')}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-black/45 transition hover:text-black"\r\n        >\r\n          <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n          Roster\r\n        </button>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">\r\n          <div className="min-w-0">\r\n            <h1 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-tight text-black">\r\n              {studentName || \'Student\'}\r\n            </h1>\r\n            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45">\r\n              {studentEmail && <span className="truncate font-mono">{studentEmail}</span>}\r\n              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">\r\n                <Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n                {isOnline ? \'Online on LSAT U\' : \'Offline\'}\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>\r\n              {lastActiveAbsolute && <span>· {lastActiveAbsolute}</span>}\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowAssign(true)}\r\n            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover"\r\n          >\r\n            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />\r\n            Assign work\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {/* Sub-tabs sit below the shell\'s room rail, so they take the ramp\'s\r\n            13px label step — subordinate to the rail\'s 14px without inventing\r\n            an undocumented size. */}\r\n        <nav className="-mb-px mt-3 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto" aria-label="Dossier sections">\r\n          {TABS.map((tab) => (\r\n            <NavLink\r\n              key={tab.to}\r\n              to={tab.to}\r\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\r\n                cn(\r\n                  \'whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition\',\r\n                  isActive\r\n                    ? \'border-black font-semibold text-black\'\r\n                    : \'border-transparent font-medium text-black/45 hover:text-black/75\',\r\n                )\r\n              }\r\n            >\r\n              {tab.label}\r\n            </NavLink>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </nav>\r\n      </header>\r\n\r\n      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">\r\n        <Outlet context={context} />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {showAssign && user && (\r\n        <AssignmentBuilder\r\n          studentId={studentId}\r\n          adminUserId={user.id}\r\n          onClose={() => setShowAssign(false)}\r\n        />\r\n      )}\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q4\nimport { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { useClassId } from "@/hooks/useClassId";\r\nimport { questionBank } from "@/lib/questionLoader";\r\nimport { localDayISO } from "@/lib/localDate";\r\nimport {\r\n  computeStudentAnalytics,\r\n  type AttemptRow,\r\n  type StudentAnalytics,\r\n} from "@/lib/mainhubAnalytics";\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentStreak {\r\n  current: number;\r\n  longest: number;\r\n  questionsToday: number;\r\n  goal: number;\r\n  goalReached: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentAnalyticsResult {\r\n  analytics: StudentAnalytics | null;\r\n  streak: StudentStreak | null;\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n  error: string | null;\r\n  refetch: () => void;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Single query surface for the signed-in student\'s real analytics. Reads only\r\n * this student\'s `attempts` (filtered by `class_id`, which equals `user.id`\r\n * per the auth invariant) plus their streak/daily-goal profile. All numbers\r\n * are derived from real rows — never mocked — so empty states are truthful.\r\n */\r\nexport function useStudentAnalytics(): StudentAnalyticsResult {\r\n  const { classId, loading: classIdLoading } = useClassId();\r\n\r\n  const query = useQuery({\r\n    queryKey: ["student-analytics", classId],\r\n    enabled: !!classId,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const [attemptsRes, profileRes, todayRes] = await Promise.all([\r\n        supabase\r\n          .from("attempts")\r\n          .select("qid, qtype, level, correct, mode, time_ms, timestamp_iso")\r\n          .eq("class_id", classId)\r\n          .order("timestamp_iso", { ascending: true }),\r\n        supabase\r\n          .from("profiles")\r\n          .select("streak_current, longest_streak, daily_goal_questions")\r\n          .eq("class_id", classId)\r\n          .maybeSingle(),\r\n        supabase\r\n          .from("daily_stats")\r\n          .select("questions_answered")\r\n          .eq("class_id", classId)\r\n          .eq("date", localDayISO())\r\n          .maybeSingle(),\r\n      ]);\r\n\r\n      if (attemptsRes.error) throw attemptsRes.error;\r\n\r\n      const rows = (attemptsRes.data ?? []) as AttemptRow[];\r\n      const analytics = computeStudentAnalytics(rows, (qid) => {\r\n        const q = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\r\n        if (!q) return undefined;\r\n        return { reasoningType: q.reasoningType, questionFeatures: q.questionFeatures };\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      const profile = profileRes.data;\r\n      const goal = profile?.daily_goal_questions ?? 5;\r\n      const questionsToday = todayRes.data?.questions_answered ?? 0;\r\n      const streak: StudentStreak = {\r\n        current: profile?.streak_current ?? 0,\r\n        longest: profile?.longest_streak ?? 0,\r\n        questionsToday,\r\n        goal,\r\n        goalReached: questionsToday >= goal,\r\n      };\r\n\r\n      return { analytics, streak };\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    analytics: query.data?.analytics ?? null,\r\n    streak: query.data?.streak ?? null,\r\n    loading: classIdLoading || query.isLoading,\r\n    error: query.error ? (query.error as Error).message : null,\r\n    refetch: () => {\r\n      void query.refetch();\r\n    },\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n'}]

const qs=["rg -n 'path=\"/(classroom|teacher|analytics|drill|history|journal|practice-exams)|path=\"(today|students|library|inbox|insights|assignments)' src/App.tsx","Get-Content src/components/classroom/shell/ClassroomLayout.tsx -TotalCount 350","Get-Content src/hooks/useStudentAssignments.ts -TotalCount 230","Get-Content src/hooks/useStudentContext.ts -TotalCount 160"];const r=await Promise.all(qs.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu",login:false,max_output_tokens:8500})));r.forEach((x,i)=>text("Q"+i+"\n"+x.output))

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q0\n273:            <Route path="/teacher/booking-passes" />\n785:                  <Route path="/teacher/reference-library" element={\n792:                  <Route path="/teacher/reference-library/:refId" element={\n801:                  <Route path="/teacher/tutor-corpus" element={\n812:                  <Route path="/teacher/booking-access" element={\n822:                  <Route path="/teacher/session-ingest" element={\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q1\nimport * as React from "react";\r\nimport {\r\n  ChevronsUpDown,\r\n  Lock,\r\n  LogOut,\r\n  Menu,\r\n  Moon,\r\n  Settings,\r\n  Sun,\r\n  UserRound,\r\n  X,\r\n} from "lucide-react";\r\nimport { toast } from "sonner";\r\nimport {\r\n  Outlet,\r\n  useLocation,\r\n  useNavigate,\r\n  useParams,\r\n} from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport { useTheme } from "@/contexts/ThemeContext";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { useClassroomItems } from "@/hooks/useClassroomItems";\r\nimport { useFlagged } from "@/hooks/useFlagged";\r\nimport { useJournalEntries } from "@/hooks/useJournalEntries";\r\nimport { useUnreadMessageCount } from "@/hooks/useUnreadMessageCount";\r\nimport { useNotifications } from "@/hooks/useNotifications";\r\nimport { useUserPermissions } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";\r\nimport { useRailPrefs } from "@/hooks/useRailPrefs";\r\nimport { resolveRailGroups, type RailItem } from "@/lib/railCatalog";\r\nimport { preloadDrillHub } from "@/lib/routePreload";\r\nimport { flagLabel } from "@/lib/flaggedService";\r\nimport {\r\n  useStudentAssignments,\r\n  useStudentTAAssignments,\r\n} from "@/hooks/useStudentAssignments";\r\nimport {\r\n  groupNotes,\r\n  isNoteUnread,\r\n  type ClassroomWorkRow,\r\n} from "@/components/classroom/classroomData";\r\nimport {\r\n  useAssignedTodoFeed,\r\n  routeWithReturnTo,\r\n} from "@/hooks/useAssignedTodoFeed";\r\nimport { useDialogOverlay } from "@/components/classroom/shell/useDialogOverlay";\r\nimport "@/components/classroom/classroom.css";\r\n\r\ntype ClassroomSection = "notes" | "work" | "flagged" | "journal";\r\n\r\nconst SECTION_TITLES: Record<ClassroomSection, string> = {\r\n  notes: "Notes and recaps",\r\n  work: "Assigned work",\r\n  flagged: "Flagged",\r\n  journal: "Wrong answers",\r\n};\r\n\r\ntype ClassroomLayoutContextValue = {\r\n  displayName: string;\r\n  setDisplayName: (name: string) => void;\r\n  testDate: string | null;\r\n  setTestDate: (date: string | null) => void;\r\n  openSection: (section: ClassroomSection) => void;\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst ClassroomLayoutContext =\r\n  React.createContext<ClassroomLayoutContextValue | null>(null);\r\n\r\n// eslint-disable-next-line react-refresh/only-export-components\r\nexport function useClassroomLayout() {\r\n  const value = React.useContext(ClassroomLayoutContext);\r\n  if (!value) {\r\n    throw new Error("useClassroomLayout must be used inside ClassroomLayout");\r\n  }\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// Optional for leaf surfaces that can also render in isolated test and preview\r\n// harnesses. Inside the classroom shell it exposes the real section drawer.\r\n// eslint-disable-next-line react-refresh/only-export-components\r\nexport function useOptionalClassroomLayout() {\r\n  return React.useContext(ClassroomLayoutContext);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction useRetainedValue<T>(value: T, canReplace: boolean) {\r\n  const ref = React.useRef(value);\r\n  if (canReplace) ref.current = value;\r\n  return ref.current;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction sectionFromPath(pathname: string): ClassroomSection | null {\r\n  if (pathname === "/journal" || pathname.startsWith("/journal/")) {\r\n    return "journal";\r\n  }\r\n  // Flagged lives outside the /classroom path but inside the same frame.\r\n  if (pathname === "/flagged" || pathname.startsWith("/flagged/")) {\r\n    return "flagged";\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    pathname === "/classroom/recaps" ||\r\n    pathname.startsWith("/classroom/item/")\r\n  ) {\r\n    return "notes";\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    pathname.startsWith("/classroom/ta/") ||\r\n    (/^\\/classroom\\/[^/]+$/.test(pathname) &&\r\n      pathname !== "/classroom/drill" &&\r\n      pathname !== "/classroom/uploads" &&\r\n      pathname !== "/classroom/account" &&\r\n      pathname !== "/classroom/recaps")\r\n  ) {\r\n    return "work";\r\n  }\r\n  return null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction PanelWash() {\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="classroom-panel-wash" aria-hidden="true">\r\n      <i className="classroom-panel-wash-light" />\r\n      <i className="classroom-panel-wash-dark" />\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction ClassroomSidebar({\r\n  displayName,\r\n  activeSection,\r\n  onSection,\r\n  onHome,\r\n  onNavigate,\r\n}: {\r\n  displayName: string;\r\n  activeSection: ClassroomSection | null;\r\n  onSection: (section: ClassroomSection) => void;\r\n  onHome: () => void;\r\n  onNavigate?: () => void;\r\n}) {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const location = useLocation();\r\n  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();\r\n  const { user, signOut } = useAuth();\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const unreadMessages = useUnreadMessageCount();\r\n  const { notifications, markSignupNotificationsRead } = useNotifications();\r\n  const unreadStudentSignups = React.useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      notifications.filter(\r\n        (notification) => notification.type === "signup" && !notification.read\r\n      ).length,\r\n    [notifications],\r\n  );\r\n  const { prefs: railPrefs } = useRailPrefs();\r\n  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const profileRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\r\n  const triggerRef = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!menuOpen) return;\r\n    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {\r\n      if (!profileRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) {\r\n        setMenuOpen(false);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\r\n      if (event.key === "Escape") {\r\n        setMenuOpen(false);\r\n        triggerRef.current?.focus();\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);\r\n    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);\r\n    return () => {\r\n      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);\r\n      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);\r\n    };\r\n  }, [menuOpen]);\r\n\r\n  const go = (path: string) => {\r\n    onNavigate?.();\r\n    navigate(path);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleSignOut = () => {\r\n    onNavigate?.();\r\n    void signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true }).then(({ error }) => {\r\n      if (error) toast.error("Sign out failed. Please try again.");\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const openStudents = () => {\r\n    go("/teacher/students");\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (\r\n      location.pathname.startsWith("/teacher/students") &&\r\n      unreadStudentSignups > 0\r\n    ) {\r\n      void markSignupNotificationsRead();\r\n    }\r\n  }, [\r\n    location.pathname,\r\n    markSignupNotificationsRead,\r\n    unreadStudentSignups,\r\n  ]);\r\n\r\n  const navClass = (active: boolean) =>\r\n    `classroom-nav${active ? " classroom-nav-active" : ""}`;\r\n\r\n  // One predicate, matching what ProtectedRoute actually enforces. Anything\r\n  // else and the rail draws a padlock the route does not honour (or the\r\n  // reverse), which the student discovers by typing the URL.\r\n  //\r\n  // `loading` is "unknown", never "denied": every flag reads false until the\r\n  // permission fetch lands, so treating that as denial padlocks the whole rail\r\n  // for a beat on every cold load.\r\n  const canAccess = (item: Pick<RailItem, "flags" | "allowSignedIn" | "requireAdmin">) =>\r\n    item.requireAdmin\r\n      ? permissions.is_admin\r\n      : permissions.loading ||\r\n        permissions.is_admin ||\r\n        item.flags.length === 0 ||\r\n        (item.allowSignedIn && !!user) ||\r\n        item.flags.some((flag) => permissions[flag]);\r\n\r\n  // `onPrefetch` fires on hover and keyboard focus, not on click: the point is\r\n  // to have the route\'s chunk parsed before the click lands, so the student\r\n  // never sees the stage fallback at all.\r\n  const navProps = (\r\n    active: boolean,\r\n    unlocked: boolean,\r\n    onClick: () => void,\r\n    onPrefetch?: () => void,\r\n  ) => ({\r\n    className: `${navClass(active && unlocked)}${unlocked ? "" : " classroom-nav-locked"}`,\r\n    disabled: !unlocked,\r\n    onClick: unlocked ? onClick : undefined,\r\n    onMouseEnter: unlocked ? onPrefetch : undefined,\r\n    onFocus: unlocked ? onPrefetch : undefined,\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  const railGroups = resolveRailGroups(railPrefs);\r\n\r\n  // Behavior stays here (it needs closures over routing + section state); the\r\n  // catalog owns what the rail contains and in what order.\r\n  const railBehavior = (\r\n    item: RailItem,\r\n  ): { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; onPrefetch?: () => void } => {\r\n    switch (item.id) {\r\n      case "home":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname === "/classroom" && !activeSection,\r\n          onClick: () => {\r\n            onNavigate?.();\r\n            onHome();\r\n          },\r\n        };\r\n      case "notes":\r\n        return { active: activeSection === "notes", onClick: () => onSection("notes") };\r\n      case "work":\r\n        return { active: activeSection === "work", onClick: () => onSection("work") };\r\n      case "drill":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname === "/classroom/drill",\r\n          onClick: () => go("/classroom/drill"),\r\n          onPrefetch: preloadDrillHub,\r\n        };\r\n      case "testing":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname.startsWith("/testing"),\r\n          onClick: () => go("/testing"),\r\n        };\r\n      case "bootcamps":\r\n        return { active: false, onClick: () => go("/bootcamps") };\r\n      case "activities":\r\n        return { active: false, onClick: () => go("/activities") };\r\n      case "journal":\r\n        return { active: activeSection === "journal", onClick: () => go("/journal") };\r\n      case "flagged":\r\n        return { active: activeSection === "flagged", onClick: () => go("/flagged") };\r\n      case "analytics":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname.startsWith("/analytics"),\r\n          onClick: () => go("/analytics"),\r\n        };\r\n      case "admissions":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname === "/classroom/admissions",\r\n          onClick: () => go("/classroom/admissions"),\r\n        };\r\n      case "history":\r\n        return {\r\n          active: location.pathname.startsWith("/history"),\r\n          onClick: () => go("/history"),\r\n        };\r\n      case "messages":\r\n        return {\r\n          active:\r\n            location.pathname === "/inbox" ||\r\n            location.pathname.startsWith("/inbox/"),\r\n          onClick: () => go("/inbox"),\r\n        };\r\n      case "schedule":\r\n        // One entry, two stage tabs. /booking is the same shell with the Book\r\n        // tab open, so it lights the same rail item. Teachers manage each\r\n        // student\'s pass from their dossier — /booking redirects them there.\r\n        return {\r\n          active:\r\n            location.pathname === "/schedule" ||\r\n            location.pathname === "/booking" ||\r\n            location.pathname.startsWith("/teacher/students"),\r\n          onClick: () => go(permissions.is_admin ? "/teacher/students" : "/schedule"),\r\n        };\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="classroom-sidebar-card">\r\n      <PanelWash />\r\n      <div className="classroom-panel-inner">\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          className="classroom-brand"\r\n          onClick={() => go("/classroom")}\r\n        >\r\n          <i>LS</i>\r\n          <span>LSAT U</span>\r\n        </button>\r\n\r\n        <nav aria-label="Classroom navigation">\r\n          {railGroups.map((group) => (\r\n            <div className="classroom-nav-group" key={group.id}>\r\n              <b>{group.title}</b>\r\n              {group.items.map((item) => {\r\n                const { active, onClick, onPrefetch } = railBehavior(item);\r\n                const unlocked = canAccess(item);\r\n                return (\r\n                  <button\r\n                    key={item.id}\r\n                    type="button"\r\n                    {...navProps(active, unlocked, onClick, onPrefetch)}\r\n                  >\r\n                    {item.label}\r\n                    {item.id === "messages" && unlocked && unreadMessages > 0 ? (\r\n                      <span className="classroom-nav-badge">\r\n                        {unreadMessages > 9 ? "9+" : unreadMessages}\r\n                        <span className="sr-only"> unread messages</span>\r\n                      </span>\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Student-scoped view of homework_assignments.\r\n * RLS restricts to rows where student_id = auth.uid().\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport type AssignmentStatus = "assigned" | "in_progress" | "completed";\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentAssignment {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  set_id: string | null;\r\n  student_id: string;\r\n  question_qids: string[];\r\n  set_title: string;\r\n  set_description: string | null;\r\n  status: AssignmentStatus;\r\n  started_at: string | null;\r\n  completed_at: string | null;\r\n  score: number | null;\r\n  assigned_by: string;\r\n  class_id: string;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst STATUS_ORDER: Record<AssignmentStatus, number> = {\r\n  in_progress: 0,\r\n  assigned: 1,\r\n  completed: 2,\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport interface UseStudentAssignmentsResult {\r\n  assignments: StudentAssignment[];\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n  error: string | null;\r\n  refresh: () => Promise<void>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useStudentAssignments(): UseStudentAssignmentsResult {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<StudentAssignment[]>([]);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    if (!user) return;\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    const { data, error: err } = await supabase\r\n      .from("homework_assignments")\r\n      .select("*")\r\n      .eq("student_id", user.id)\r\n      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });\r\n    if (err) {\r\n      console.error("[homework:assign:student:list] failed", {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        error: err,\r\n      });\r\n      setError(err.message);\r\n      setAssignments([]);\r\n    } else {\r\n      const rows = ((data || []) as unknown) as StudentAssignment[];\r\n      rows.sort((a, b) => {\r\n        const s = STATUS_ORDER[a.status] - STATUS_ORDER[b.status];\r\n        if (s !== 0) return s;\r\n        return new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime();\r\n      });\r\n      setAssignments(rows);\r\n      setError(null);\r\n    }\r\n    setLoading(false);\r\n  }, [user]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (user) void refresh();\r\n  }, [user, refresh]);\r\n\r\n  return { assignments, loading, error, refresh };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useStudentAssignment(assignmentId: string | undefined): {\r\n  assignment: StudentAssignment | null;\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n  error: string | null;\r\n  refresh: () => Promise<void>;\r\n} {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [assignment, setAssignment] = useState<StudentAssignment | null>(null);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    if (!user || !assignmentId) return;\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    const { data, error: err } = await supabase\r\n      .from("homework_assignments")\r\n      .select("*")\r\n      .eq("id", assignmentId)\r\n      .eq("student_id", user.id)\r\n      .maybeSingle();\r\n    if (err) {\r\n      console.error("[homework:assign:student:get] failed", {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        assignmentId,\r\n        error: err,\r\n      });\r\n      setError(err.message);\r\n      setAssignment(null);\r\n    } else {\r\n      setAssignment((data as StudentAssignment) || null);\r\n      setError(null);\r\n    }\r\n    setLoading(false);\r\n  }, [user, assignmentId]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    void refresh();\r\n  }, [refresh]);\r\n\r\n  return { assignment, loading, error, refresh };\r\n}\r\n\r\n// --- TA assignments --------------------------------------------------------\r\n// ta_assignments is populated by DraftCard\'s approve flow. RLS on the table\r\n// restricts students to rows where student_id = auth.uid(); admins have full\r\n// CRUD. Kept in this file per the Gap 1 brief so callers have one import.\r\n\r\nexport type TAAssignmentStatus = "assigned" | "viewed" | "completed";\r\n\r\nexport interface TAResponseField {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  kind?: "short" | "long";\r\n  rows?: number;\r\n  placeholder?: string;\r\n  required?: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface TAResponseSection {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  instructions?: string;\r\n  fields: TAResponseField[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface TAResponseSchema {\r\n  version: 1;\r\n  sections: TAResponseSection[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentTAAssignment {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  student_id: string;\r\n  interaction_id: string | null;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  content_html: string;\r\n  asset_ids: string[];\r\n  pdf_url: string | null;\r\n  status: TAAssignmentStatus;\r\n  assigned_by: string;\r\n  assigned_at: string;\r\n  viewed_at: string | null;\r\n  completed_at: string | null;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  response_schema?: TAResponseSchema;\r\n  response_data?: Record<string, string>;\r\n  response_saved_at?: string | null;\r\n  answer_key_html?: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst TA_STATUS_ORDER: Record<TAAssignmentStatus, number> = {\r\n  assigned: 0,\r\n  viewed: 1,\r\n  completed: 2,\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport function useStudentTAAssignments(): {\r\n  assignments: StudentTAAssignment[];\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n  error: string | null;\r\n  refresh: () => Promise<void>;\r\n} {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<StudentTAAssignment[]>([]);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    if (!user) return;\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    const { data, error: err } = await supabase\r\n      .from("ta_assignments")\r\n      .select("*")\r\n      .eq("student_id", user.id)\r\n      .order("assigned_at", { ascending: false });\r\n    if (err) {\r\n      console.error("[ta:assign:student:list] failed", {\r\n        userId: user.id,\r\n        error: err,\r\n      });\r\n      setError(err.message);\r\n      setAssignments([]);\r\n    } else {\r\n      const rows = ((data || []) as unknown) as StudentTAAssignment[];\r\n      rows.sort((a, b) => {\r\n        const s = TA_STATUS_ORDER[a.status] - TA_STATUS_ORDER[b.status];\r\n        if (s !== 0) return s;\r\n        return new Date(b.assigned_at).getTime() - new Date(a.assigned_at).getTime();\r\n      });\r\n      setAssignments(rows);\r\n      setError(null);\r\n    }\r\n    setLoading(false);\r\n  }, [user]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (user) void refresh();\r\n  }, [user, refresh]);\r\n\r\n  return { assignments, loading, error, refresh };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Admin-scoped list of a specific student\'s TA assignments.\r\n * RLS on `ta_assignments` grants admins full read; students only see their own.\r\n */\r\nexport function useAdminTAAssignmentsForStudent(studentId: string | null | undefined): {\r\n  assignments: StudentTAAssignment[];\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q3\nimport { useEffect } from "react";\r\nimport { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport type { Json } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport { extractPdfText } from "@/lib/pdfExtract";\r\n\r\nexport type StudentContextType =\r\n  | "file"\r\n  | "screenshot"\r\n  | "transcript"\r\n  | "note"\r\n  | "external_score";\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentContextItem {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  student_id: string;\r\n  context_type: StudentContextType;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  content_text: string | null;\r\n  file_path: string | null;\r\n  source: string | null;\r\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\r\n  created_by: string;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst BUCKET = "student-context";\r\nconst MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024;\r\nconst MAX_TEXT_CHARS = 200_000;\r\n\r\nconst contextTable = () => supabase.from("student_context");\r\n\r\nexport function useStudentContext(studentId: string) {\r\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\r\n  const query = useQuery<StudentContextItem[]>({\r\n    queryKey: ["student-context", studentId],\r\n    enabled: !!studentId,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await contextTable()\r\n        .select("*")\r\n        .eq("student_id", studentId)\r\n        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        console.error("[useStudentContext] fetch failed:", error);\r\n        throw error;\r\n      }\r\n      return (data ?? []) as StudentContextItem[];\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Realtime: refresh when any row changes for this student. Lets a\r\n  // second open tab (or the AttachmentBar uploading from the chat)\r\n  // reflect immediately in the Hub\'s Notes tab. The channel name\r\n  // includes a random suffix so multi-mount doesn\'t collide.\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!studentId) return;\r\n    const channel = supabase\r\n      .channel(`student-context-${studentId}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`)\r\n      .on(\r\n        "postgres_changes",\r\n        {\r\n          event: "*",\r\n          schema: "public",\r\n          table: "student_context",\r\n          filter: `student_id=eq.${studentId}`,\r\n        },\r\n        () => {\r\n          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["student-context", studentId] });\r\n        }\r\n      )\r\n      .subscribe();\r\n    return () => {\r\n      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\r\n    };\r\n  }, [studentId, qc]);\r\n\r\n  return query;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface UploadFileInput {\r\n  studentId: string;\r\n  file: File;\r\n  contextType: Extract<StudentContextType, "file" | "screenshot" | "transcript">;\r\n  title?: string;\r\n  source?: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function extractTextFromFile(file: File): Promise<string> {\r\n  if (file.type === "application/pdf") {\r\n    return extractPdfText(file);\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    file.type.startsWith("text/") ||\r\n    file.name.endsWith(".txt") ||\r\n    file.name.endsWith(".md")\r\n  ) {\r\n    return (await file.text()).slice(0, MAX_TEXT_CHARS);\r\n  }\r\n  // Images and other binary types — store without text.\r\n  return "";\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useUploadStudentContextFile() {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\r\n  return useMutation({\r\n    mutationFn: async (input: UploadFileInput) => {\r\n      if (!user) throw new Error("Not signed in");\r\n      const { studentId, file, contextType } = input;\r\n      if (file.size > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {\r\n        throw new Error(\r\n          `File too large (max ${MAX_FILE_BYTES / 1024 / 1024} MB)`\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const contentText = await extractTextFromFile(file);\r\n      const title = (input.title?.trim() || file.name).slice(0, 200);\r\n\r\n      // Insert row first so we have an id to namespace the storage path.\r\n      const { data: inserted, error: insertError } = await contextTable()\r\n        .insert({\r\n          student_id: studentId,\r\n          context_type: contextType,\r\n          title,\r\n          content_text: contentText || null,\r\n          file_path: null,\r\n          source: input.source ?? "upload",\r\n          metadata: { size: file.size, mime: file.type || null } as Json,\r\n          created_by: user.id,\r\n        })\r\n        .select("id")\r\n        .single();\r\n      if (insertError) throw insertError;\r\n      const id = (inserted as { id: string }).id;\r\n\r\n      // Upload to storage; roll back the row if the upload fails so we\r\n      // never leave a dangling context record.\r\n      const safeName = file.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_");\r\n      const path = `${studentId}/${id}/${safeName}`;\r\n      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage\r\n        .from(BUCKET)\r\n        .upload(path, file, { upsert: false });\r\n      if (uploadError) {\r\n        await contextTable().delete().eq("id", id);\r\n        throw uploadError;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const { error: updateError } = await contextTable()\r\n        .update({ file_path: path })\r\n        .eq("id", id);\r\n      if (updateError) throw updateError;\r\n\r\n      return { id, title };\r\n    },\r\n    onSuccess: (_res, vars) => {\r\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["student-context", vars.studentId] });\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n'}]

Every route drawn by it hangs off\r\n                      THIS ONE pathless layout route, which is what keeps the\r\n                      rail mounted across navigations — two sibling routes each\r\n                      holding their own <ClassroomLayout /> would unmount and\r\n                      remount it, which is exactly the shell-swap this design\r\n                      exists to eliminate.\r\n\r\n                      Each branch keeps its own guard, so /flagged still needs\r\n                      has_flagged_access and nothing about who can reach what\r\n                      has changed. */}\r\n                  <Route element={<ClassroomLayout />}>\r\n                    <Route path="/classroom" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access" allowSignedIn><Outlet /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    }>\r\n                      <Route index element={<Classroom />} />\r\n                      <Route path="drill" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute flag="has_drill_access">\r\n                          <AcademyMainhub />\r\n                        </ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                      <Route path="uploads" element={<ClassroomUploadsView />} />\r\n                      <Route path="account" element={<ClassroomAccountView />} />\r\n                      {/* The outlet above lets any signed-in user through so a\r\n                          fresh signup can reach the intake and their account.\r\n                          Coursework does not ride on that: notes, recaps and\r\n                          assigned work are what has_classroom_access buys. */}\r\n                      <Route path="recaps" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access"><ClassroomRecapsView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                      {/* Admissions is unfinished. Keep the rail entry visible,\r\n                          but only Joshua and admins can open the page. */}\r\n                      <Route path="admissions" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute requireAdmin>\r\n                          <AdmissionsPage />\r\n                        </ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                      <Route path="item/:itemId" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access"><ClassroomItemView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                      <Route path="ta/:assignmentId" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access"><ClassroomTAAssignmentView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                      <Route path=":assignmentId" element={\r\n                        <ProtectedRoute flag="has_classroom_access"><ClassroomAssignmentView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                      } />\r\n                    </Route>\r\n\r\n                    {/* The Testing Centre sits inside the classroom frame on\r\n                        purpose: choosing a section is still classroom time, and\r\n                        the rail has to stay reachable right up to the moment the\r\n                        clock starts. The runners themselves are full-screen\r\n                        takeovers registered above, outside this shell. */}\r\n                    <Route path="/testing" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_drill_access"><TestingCentre /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n                    <Route path="/testing/test/:runId" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_drill_access"><PracticeTestRunPage /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n\r\n                    {/* Flagged keeps its own URL and its own place in the rail;\r\n                        only the frame that draws it has changed. */}\r\n                    <Route path="/flagged" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_flagged_access"><FlaggedIndexView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n                    <Route path="/flagged/:qid" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_flagged_access"><FlaggedQuestionView /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n                    <Route path="/journal" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag={["has_journal_access", "has_waj_access"]}>\r\n                        <JournalIndexView />\r\n                      </ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n                    <Route path="/journal/:qid" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag={["has_journal_access", "has_waj_access"]}>\r\n                        <JournalEntryView />\r\n                      </ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n\r\n                    {/* Messages is reached from the classroom rail\'s Support\r\n                        group, so it draws inside this frame. Routing it under\r\n                        StudentShell swapped the whole rail out from under the\r\n                        user mid-navigation. */}\r\n                    {/* One splat route, not /inbox + /inbox/:conversationId as\r\n                        siblings: two Route elements make React unmount and\r\n                        remount Inbox on every list↔thread move, which flashes\r\n                        the skeleton and re-runs the whole inbox load each time\r\n                        a thread is opened or backed out of. */}\r\n                    <Route path="/inbox/*" element={\r\n                      <ProtectedRoute flag="has_chat_access"><Inbox /></ProtectedRoute>\r\n                    } />\r\n\r\n                    {/* These five were the last routes under StudentShell, so\r\n                        reaching any of them from the rail swapped the whole\r\n                        frame: a different sidebar, a different item set, and\r\n                        each page\'s own header on top.
