\'@tanstack/react-query\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { useStudentAssignments, useStudentTAAssignments } from \'@/hooks/useStudentAssignments\';\r\nimport { useClassroomItems, type ClassroomItem } from \'@/hooks/useClassroomItems\';\r\nimport { resolveItemRoute } from \'@/lib/classroomItems\';\r\nimport { readSectionBriefing, type SectionBriefing } from \'@/lib/sectionBriefing\';\r\nimport { listAssignmentsForStudent as listGuidedForStudent, type GuidedDrill, type GuidedDrillAssignment } from \'@/lib/guidedDrills\';\r\nimport { listStudentAssignments as listActivityForStudent } from \'@/features/activity-centre/api/activitiesClient\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * A unified read-only "assigned" row surfaced in the student\'s Today list.\r\n * Status is derived from the underlying source row so completing work on the\r\n * assignment page reflects here without any extra plumbing.\r\n */\r\nexport interface AssignedTodoRow {\r\n  key: string;\r\n  kind: \'homework\' | \'ta\' | \'activity\' | \'guided\' | \'bootcamp\' | \'plan\';\r\n  refId: string;\r\n  /** The table `refId` points into, so a classroom_items row can be matched to it. */\r\n  sourceTable: string;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  subtitle: string;\r\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\r\n  createdAt: string;\r\n  /** Due date, when a classroom_items row carries one. */\r\n  dueAt: string | null;\r\n  route: string;\r\n  /**\r\n   * Work that reports its own completion, so the student is never asked to\r\n   * assert it. A timed section is the case: sitting it IS finishing it, and a\r\n   * "Mark done" button beside one can be pressed without doing the section at\r\n   * all, which is worse than useless on work assigned as a measurement.\r\n   */\r\n  selfCompleting?: boolean;\r\n  /**\r\n   * Present only on a timed section. Home opens these instructions over the\r\n   * stage instead of navigating, so declining costs the student nothing.\r\n   */\r\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\r\n}\r\n\r\ntype GuidedRow = GuidedDrillAssignment & { guided_drill: GuidedDrill };\r\n\r\nfunction normalizeStatus(raw: string | null | undefined): AssignedTodoRow[\'status\'] {\r\n  if (raw === \'completed\') return \'completed\';\r\n  if (raw === \'assigned\') return \'assigned\';\r\n  return \'in_progress\';\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** classroom_items types that represent work rather than reading material. */\r\nconst WORK_ITEM_KIND: Partial<Record<ClassroomItem[\'type\'], AssignedTodoRow[\'kind\']>> = {\r\n  drill: \'guided\',\r\n  homework: \'homework\',\r\n  activity: \'activity\',\r\n  bootcamp_module: \'bootcamp\',\r\n  weekly_plan_task: \'plan\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * `/guided/:id` reads `returnTo` to decide where finishing the drill lands the\r\n * student. Building it into the row would bake in whichever surface happened to\r\n * construct the feed, which is how every guided drill ended up returning to\r\n * `/schedule` no matter where it was launched from. Consumers call this at\r\n * click time with their own location instead.\r\n */\r\nexport function routeWithReturnTo(route: string, from: string): string {\r\n  if (!route.startsWith(\'/guided/\')) return route;\r\n  const [path, query = \'\'] = route.split(\'?\');\r\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);\r\n  params.set(\'returnTo\', from);\r\n  return `${path}?${params.toString()}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The single source for "what has been assigned to me".\r\n *\r\n * Two independent builders used to answer this question — this hook, and\r\n * `buildWorkRows` in classroomData.ts — reading overlapping but different\r\n * tables. The result was not merely inconsistent, it was disjoint: Home listed\r\n * guided drills and no bootcamp, the Classroom\'s Assigned work panel listed the\r\n * bootcamp and no drills. Everything now merges here.\r\n *\r\n * Identity is `sourceTable:refId`. A classroom_items row that points at a\r\n * source assignment is the preferred representation of it — the teacher can\r\n * retitle it and set a due date — so it overrides, and `resolveItemRoute` sends\r\n * it to the same destination the raw row would have used.\r\n */\r\nexport interface AssignedFeedSources {\r\n  hw: ReturnType<typeof useStudentAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\r\n  ta: ReturnType<typeof useStudentTAAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\r\n  guided: GuidedRow[];\r\n  activities: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listActivityForStudent>>;\r\n  items: ClassroomItem[];\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The pure merge behind the hook, exported so the disjoint-feed and\r\n * duplicate-assignment rules can be tested without standing up five queries.\r\n */\r\nexport function buildAssignedRows({\r\n  hw,\r\n  ta,\r\n  guided,\r\n  activities,\r\n  items,\r\n}: AssignedFeedSources): AssignedTodoRow[] {\r\n  {\r\n    const byIdentity = new Map<string, AssignedTodoRow>();\r\n    const put = (row: AssignedTodoRow) => byIdentity.set(`${row.sourceTable}:${row.refId}`, row);\r\n\r\n    for (const a of hw) {\r\n      put({\r\n        key: `hw-${a.id}`,\r\n        kind: \'homework\',\r\n        refId: a.id,\r\n        sourceTable: \'homework_assignments\',\r\n        title: a.set_title || \'Homework\',\r\n        subtitle: `${a.question_qids?.length ?? 0} questions${a.score != null ? ` · ${Math.round(a.score)}%` : \'\'}`,\r\n        status: normalizeStatus(a.status),\r\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\r\n        dueAt: null,\r\n        route: `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const a of ta) {\r\n      put({\r\n        key: `ta-${a.id}`,\r\n        kind: \'ta\',\r\n        refId: a.id,\r\n        sourceTable: \'ta_assignments\',\r\n        title: a.title || \'From Joshua\',\r\n        subtitle: a.pdf_url ? \'PDF attached\' : \'TA assignment\',\r\n        status: a.status === \'viewed\' ? \'in_progress\' : normalizeStatus(a.status),\r\n        createdAt: a.assigned_at,\r\n        dueAt: null,\r\n        route: `/classroom/ta/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Assigning the same drill twice writes two rows that render identically,\r\n    // so they collapse to one. An open assignment always wins over a completed\r\n    // one — otherwise finishing the older twin would hide work still owed —\r\n    // and the newest wins within that.\r\n    const bestByDrill = new Map<string, GuidedRow>();\r\n    for (const g of guided) {\r\n      const drillId = g.guided_drill_id ?? g.id;\r\n      const held = bestByDrill.get(drillId);\r\n      if (!held) {\r\n        bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n      const heldOpen = held.status !== \'completed\';\r\n      const thisOpen = g.status !== \'completed\';\r\n      if (thisOpen !== heldOpen) {\r\n        if (thisOpen) bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n      if (new Date(g.assigned_at).getTime() > new Date(held.assigned_at).getTime()) {\r\n        bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    for (const g of bestByDrill.values()) {\r\n      put({\r\n        key: `g-${g.id}`,\r\n        kind: \'guided\',\r\n        refId: g.id,\r\n        sourceTable: \'guided_drill_assignments\',\r\n        title: g.guided_drill?.title || \'Guided drill\',\r\n        subtitle: `${g.guided_drill?.question_count ?? \'?\'} questions${g.guided_drill?.estimated_minutes ? ` · ${g.guided_drill.estimated_minutes} min` : \'\'}`,\r\n        status: g.status === \'completed\' ? \'completed\' : g.status === \'assigned\' ? \'assigned\' : \'in_progress\',\r\n        createdAt: g.assigned_at,\r\n        dueAt: null,\r\n        route: `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(g.id)}`,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const a of activities) {\r\n      put({\r\n        key: `act-${a.id}`,\r\n        kind: \'activity\',\r\n        refId: a.id,\r\n        sourceTable: \'activity_assignments\',\r\n        title: a.title || \'Activity\',\r\n        subtitle: a.subtitle || \'Activity\',\r\n        status: normalizeStatus(a.status),\r\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\r\n        dueAt: null,\r\n        route: `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const item of items) {\r\n      const kind = WORK_ITEM_KIND[item.type];\r\n      if (!kind) continue; // notes and recaps are reading material, not work\r\n      const route = resolveItemRoute(item);\r\n      if (!route) continue;\r\n      const sourceTable = item.source_table ?? \'classroom_items\';\r\n      const refId = item.source_id ?? item.id;\r\n      const existing = byIdentity.get(`${sourceTable}:${refId}`);\r\n      // Completion wins over the mirror. A student who finished the underlying\r\n      // assignment must not see it come back because the classroom_items row it\r\n      // was mirrored into still says "assigned" — that is why finished work kept\r\n      // reappearing on Home.\r\n      const itemStatus = normalizeStatus(item.status);\r\n      const status =\r\n        itemStatus === \'completed\' || existing?.status === \'completed\' ? \'completed\' : itemStatus;\r\n      byIdentity.set(`${sourceTable}:${refId}`, {\r\n        key: `ci-${item.id}`,\r\n        kind,\r\n        refId,\r\n        sourceTable,\r\n        title: item.title || existing?.title || \'Assigned work\',\r\n        // The item carries no question count, so keep the source row\'s subtitle\r\n        // when there is one.\r\n        subtitle: existing?.subtitle ?? item.description ?? \'\',\r\n        status,\r\n        createdAt: item.created_at,\r\n        dueAt: item.due_at,\r\n        route,\r\n        selfCompleting:\r\n          (item.metadata_json as { kind?: string } | null)?.kind === \'timed_section\',\r\n        briefing: readSectionBriefing(item.metadata_json) ?? undefined,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Open work first, then completed; newest within each bucket.\r\n    return [...byIdentity.values()].sort((x, y) => {\r\n      const s = (x.status === \'completed\' ? 1 : 0) - (y.status === \'completed\' ? 1 : 0);\r\n      if (s !== 0) return s;\r\n      return new Date(y.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(x.createdAt).getTime();\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** @see buildAssignedRows — this hook only supplies it with live queries. */\r\nexport function useAssignedTodoFeed(): {\r\n  rows: AssignedTodoRow[];\r\n  loading: boolean;\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\n/**\r\n * Mainhub analytics engine.\r\n *\r\n * Pure, dependency-free reducers over raw `attempts` rows for a single\r\n * signed-in student. The `attempts` table is the atomic "every completed\r\n * question interaction" unit: one row per answered question across Smart\r\n * Drill, Full Section, Build a Set, bootcamp drills, and blind review.\r\n *\r\n * Diagnostic rule (locked in the design spec): the DEFAULT accuracy number\r\n * everywhere is FIRST-ATTEMPT accuracy — the earliest attempt per `qid`.\r\n * Repeat/review attempts are kept and surfaced as a secondary metric so\r\n * retries never inflate the diagnostic picture.\r\n *\r\n * No data source beyond the student\'s own `attempts` rows (plus a caller-\r\n * supplied question resolver for reasoning-type / feature / difficulty\r\n * metadata that lives in the question bank, not the attempt row). When a\r\n * dimension lacks enough first attempts, callers render a truthful empty /\r\n * low-sample state rather than a populated chart.\r\n */\r\n\r\n/** Raw shape we read from the `attempts` table (student-scoped). */\r\nexport interface AttemptRow {\r\n  qid: string;\r\n  qtype: string;\r\n  level: number;\r\n  correct: boolean;\r\n  mode: string | null;\r\n  time_ms: number | null;\r\n  timestamp_iso: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Question-bank metadata joined by qid (reasoning types + features). */\r\nexport interface QuestionMeta {\r\n  reasoningType?: string[];\r\n  questionFeatures?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type QuestionResolver = (qid: string) => QuestionMeta | undefined;\r\n\r\n/** Minimum first attempts before a dimension\'s accuracy is considered trustworthy. */\r\nexport const MIN_SAMPLE = 3;\r\n/** Full-confidence sample size for the weighted priority score. */\r\nexport const CONFIDENCE_SATURATION = 8;\r\n/** "Near-mastery" target; the gap below it drives the priority score. */\r\nexport const TARGET_ACCURACY = 85;\r\n\r\nexport interface DimensionStat {\r\n  key: string;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  /** Distinct questions with a first attempt in this dimension. */\r\n  firstAttempts: number;\r\n  firstCorrect: number;\r\n  /** 0-100. null when firstAttempts === 0. */\r\n  firstAccuracy: number | null;\r\n  /** Attempts beyond the first, on already-seen questions. */\r\n  repeatAttempts: number;\r\n  repeatCorrect: number;\r\n  /** 0-100. null when repeatAttempts === 0. */\r\n  repeatAccuracy: number | null;\r\n  /** Avg time over first attempts, ms. null when unavailable. */\r\n  avgTimeMs: number | null;\r\n  /** Avg difficulty (level) over first attempts. null when unavailable. */\r\n  avgDifficulty: number | null;\r\n  /** Whether firstAttempts >= MIN_SAMPLE. */\r\n  reliable: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PriorityRow extends DimensionStat {\r\n  /** Weighted weakness score (higher = more urgent). */\r\n  score: number;\r\n  /** Share of this dimension\'s first attempts that were misses in the last 14 days. */\r\n  recentMissRate: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface TrendPoint {\r\n  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD\r\n  accuracy: number; // 0-100, first-attempt\r\n  count: number; // first attempts that day\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface VolumePoint {\r\n  /** ISO date of the bucket start (day). */\r\n  date: string;\r\n  attempts: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentAnalytics {\r\n  totalAttempts: number;\r\n  /** Distinct questions attempted. */\r\n  totalQuestions: number;\r\n  firstAttemptAccuracy: number | null;\r\n  repeatAccuracy: number | null;\r\n  /** last-7d minus prior-7d first-attempt accuracy, in points. null when insufficient. */\r\n  trendDelta: number | null;\r\n  accuracyTrend: TrendPoint[];\r\n  volumeByDay: VolumePoint[];\r\n  byType: DimensionStat[];\r\n  byDifficulty: DimensionStat[];\r\n  byReasoning: DimensionStat[];\r\n  byFeature: DimensionStat[];\r\n  priorities: PriorityRow[];\r\n  /** True when there is at least one attempt. */\r\n  hasData: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\ninterface FirstAttemptFlag {\r\n  row: AttemptRow;\r\n  isFirst: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst DAY_MS = 86_400_000;\r\n\r\nfunction dayKey(iso: string | null): string | null {\r\n  if (!iso) return null;\r\n  const d = new Date(iso);\r\n  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;\r\n  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Flags each row as a first attempt (earliest timestamp for its qid) or a\r\n * repeat. Rows without a timestamp sort last and are treated as repeats if a\r\n * timestamped attempt for the same qid exists.\r\n */\r\nfunction flagFirstAttempts(rows: AttemptRow[]): FirstAttemptFlag[] {\r\n  const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) => {\r\n    const ta = a.timestamp_iso ? Date.parse(a.timestamp_iso) : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\r\n    const tb = b.timestamp_iso ? Date.parse(b.timestamp_iso) : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\r\n    return ta - tb;\r\n  });\r\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\r\n  return sorted.map((row) => {\r\n    const isFirst = !seen.has(row.qid);\r\n    seen.add(row.qid);\r\n    return { row, isFirst };\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\ninterface Accum {\r\n  firstAttempts: number;\r\n  firstCorrect: number;\r\n  repeatAttempts: number;\r\n  repeatCorrect: number;\r\n  timeSum: number;\r\n  timeCount: number;\r\n  difficultySum: number;\r\n  difficultyCount: number;\r\n  recentFirst: number;\r\n  recentFirstMiss: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction emptyAccum(): Accum {\r\n  return {\r\n    firstAttempts: 0,\r\n    firstCorrect: 0,\r\n    repeatAttempts: 0,\r\n    repeatCorrect: 0,\r\n    timeSum: 0,\r\n    timeCount: 0,\r\n    difficultySum: 0,\r\n    difficultyCount: 0,\r\n    recentFirst: 0,\r\n    recentFirstMiss: 0,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction addToAccum(acc: Accum, flag: FirstAttemptFlag, recentCutoff: number): void {\r\n  const { row, isFirst } = flag;\r\n  const ts = row.timestamp_iso ? Date.parse(row.timestamp_iso) : NaN;\r\n  if (isFirst) {\r\n    acc.firstAttempts += 1;\r\n    if (row.correct) acc.firstCorrect += 1;\r\n    if (typeof row.time_ms === "number" && row.time_ms > 0) {\r\n      acc.timeSum += row.time_ms;\r\n      acc.timeCount += 1;\r\n    }\r\n    if (typeof row.level === "number" && Number.isFinite(row.level)) {\r\n      acc.difficultySum += row.level;\r\n      acc.difficultyCount += 1;\r\n    }\r\n    if (!Number.isNaN(ts) && ts >= recentCutoff) {\r\n      acc.recentFirst += 1;\r\n      if (!row.correct) acc.recentFirstMiss += 1;\r\n    }\r\n  } else {\r\n    acc.repeatAttempts += 1;\r\n    if (row.correct) acc.repeatCorrect += 1;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction finalizeStat(key: string, label: string, acc: Accum): DimensionStat {\r\n  return {\r\n    key,\r\n    label,\r\n    firstAttempts: acc.firstAttempts,\r\n    firstCorrect: acc.firstCorrect,\r\n    firstAccuracy: acc.firstAttempts > 0 ? (acc.firstCorrect / acc.firstAttempts) * 100 : null,\r\n    repeatAttempts: acc.repeatAttempts,\r\n    repeatCorrect: acc.repeatCorrect,\r\n    repeatAccuracy: acc.repeatAttempts > 0 ? (acc.repeatCorrect / acc.repeatAttempts) * 100 : null,\r\n    avgTimeMs: acc.timeCount > 0 ? acc.timeSum / acc.timeCount : null,\r\n    avgDifficulty: acc.difficultyCount > 0 ? acc.difficultySum / acc.difficultyCount : null,\r\n    reliable: acc.firstAttempts >= MIN_SAMPLE,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Weighted weakness score. Combines, per the spec: low first-attempt\r\n * accuracy, enough sample to trust it, and recent misses weighted more than\r\n * old ones. A dimension with 20 attempts at 48% ranks above one with 1\r\n * attempt at 0%.\r\n */\r\nfunction priorityScore(acc: Accum): number {\r\n  if (acc.firstAttempts < MIN_SAMPLE) return 0;\r\n  const firstAccuracy = (acc.firstCorrect / acc.firstAttempts) * 100;\r\n  const gap = Math.max(0, TARGET_ACCURACY - firstAccuracy);\r\n  const confidence = Math.min(1, acc.firstAttempts / CONFIDENCE_SATURATION);\r\n  const recentMissRate = acc.recentFirst > 0 ? acc.recentFirstMiss / acc.recentFirst : 0;\r\n  const recencyBoost = 1 + recentMissRate; // 1x .. 2x\r\n  return Math.round(gap * confidence * recencyBoost);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction buildDimension(\r\n  flags: FirstAttemptFlag[],\r\n  recentCutoff: number,\r\n  keysFor: (flag: FirstAttemptFlag) => Array<{ key: string; label: string }>,\r\n): DimensionStat[] {\r\n  const map = new Map<string, { label: string; acc: Accum }>();\r\n  for (const flag of flags) {\r\n    for (const { key, label } of keysFor(flag)) {\r\n      let entry = map.get(key);\r\n      if (!entry) {\r\n        entry = { label, acc: emptyAccum() };\r\n        map.set(key, entry);\r\n      }\r\n      addToAccum(entry.acc, flag, recentCutoff);\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  return Array.from(map.entries())\r\n    .map(([key, { label, acc }]) => finalizeStat(key, label, acc))\r\n    .sort((a, b) => b.firstAttempts - a.firstAttempts);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Core reducer. `now` is injectable for deterministic tests.\r\n */\r\nexport function computeStudentAnalytics(\r\n  rows: AttemptRow[],\r\n  resolve: QuestionResolver,\r\n  now: number = Date.now(),\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q3\nimport { Plus } from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport HubAssignments from \'@/components/hub/HubAssignments\';\r\nimport { BootcampLessonProgress } from \'@/components/teacher/bootcamps/BootcampLessonProgress\';\r\nimport { StudentIndicatorLedger } from \'@/features/activity-centre/components/StudentIndicatorLedger\';\r\nimport { useDossier } from \'./StudentDossier\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Work = every assignment kind for this student in one place.\r\n *\r\n * This reuses `HubAssignments`, which already queries all three real sources\r\n * (`homework_assignments`, `ta_assignments`, `classroom_items`) and carries\r\n * the unassign/delete flows. It groups by kind rather than interleaving one\r\n * newest-first list; each group is already newest-first. Rebuilding it as a\r\n * single interleaved timeline would have meant re-deriving those queries and\r\n * dropping the delete affordances, so the grouping stands until there is a\r\n * reason to trade that away.\r\n */\r\nexport default function DossierWork() {\r\n  const { studentId, openAssign } = useDossier();\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="space-y-4 p-5">\r\n      <div className="flex justify-end">\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          onClick={openAssign}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover"\r\n        >\r\n          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />\r\n          Assign work\r\n        </button>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <HubAssignments studentId={studentId} />\r\n\r\n      {/* Renders nothing when there is no stored progress, so it never adds an\r\n          empty block. */}\r\n      <BootcampLessonProgress studentClassId={studentId} />\r\n\r\n      {/* What their Activity work says to teach next, read from the attempts\r\n          they have already made. Also renders nothing when there are none. */}\r\n      <StudentIndicatorLedger studentClassId={studentId} />\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "Q0\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient, useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';\r\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\r\n\r\nexport interface AnalyticsSource {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  student_id: string;\r\n  source_name: '7sage' | 'lawhub' | 'other';\r\n  source_url: string | null;\r\n  embed_status: 'unknown' | 'allowed' | 'blocked' | 'external_only';\r\n  last_imported_at: string | null;\r\n  notes: string | null;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  updated_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useAnalyticsSources(studentId: string | null) {\r\n  return useQuery<AnalyticsSource[]>({\r\n    queryKey: ['analytics-sources', studentId],\r\n    enabled: !!studentId,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('student_analytics_sources')\r\n        .select('*')\r\n        .eq('student_id', studentId)\r\n        .order('source_name');\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return (data ?? []) as AnalyticsSource[];\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useUpsertAnalyticsSource() {\r\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\r\n  return useMutation({\r\n    mutationFn: async (row: Partial<AnalyticsSource> & { student_id: string; source_name: string }) => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('student_analytics_sources')\r\n        .upsert(row, { onConflict: 'student_id,source_name' })\r\n        .select()\r\n        .single();\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['analytics-sources'] });\r\n      return data as AnalyticsSource;\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q1\nimport * as React from \'react\';\r\nimport { useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { Button } from \'@/components/ui/button\';\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport { useClassId } from \'@/hooks/useClassId\';\r\nimport { questionBank } from \'@/lib/questionLoader\';\r\nimport { computeAnalyticsFromRollup, type AnalyticsData, type OpportunityArea } from \'@/lib/analyticsRollup\';\r\nimport { useScrollAnimation } from \'@/hooks/useScrollAnimation\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\nimport { useTheme } from \'@/contexts/ThemeContext\';\r\nimport BreakdownPanel from \'@/components/analytics/BreakdownPanel\';\r\nimport FlaggedQuestionsList from \'@/components/analytics/FlaggedQuestionsList\';\r\nimport ImportedMetricsPanel from \'@/components/analytics/ImportedMetricsPanel\';\r\nimport { Flag } from \'lucide-react\';\r\n\r\nconst Analytics = () => {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const { theme } = useTheme();\r\n  const isLight = theme === \'light\';\r\n  const [data, setData] = React.useState<AnalyticsData | null>(null);\r\n  const [opportunities, setOpportunities] = React.useState<OpportunityArea[]>([]);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);\r\n  const [loadError, setLoadError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const { classId, loading: classIdLoading } = useClassId();\r\n  const opportunitiesRef = useScrollAnimation();\r\n  const performanceRef = useScrollAnimation();\r\n\r\n  // SVG color helpers — adapt to current theme\r\n  const svgTrack   = isLight ? \'rgba(0,0,0,0.07)\'  : \'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)\';\r\n  const svgArc     = isLight ? \'rgba(0,0,0,0.65)\'  : \'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)\';\r\n  const svgArcSm   = isLight ? \'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)\'  : \'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)\';\r\n  const dotColor   = isLight ? \'rgb(0,0,0)\'         : \'rgb(255,255,255)\';\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!user) {\r\n      navigate(\'/auth\');\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    if (classIdLoading || !classId) return;\r\n    loadAnalytics();\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [user, classId, classIdLoading]);\r\n\r\n  const loadAnalytics = async () => {\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    setLoadError(null);\r\n    try {\r\n      // Server-side qtype x level x window rollup (attempt_accuracy_rollup) instead\r\n      // of downloading every attempt row and bucketing client-side. Each row is\r\n      // { qtype, level, bucket, total, correct } where bucket is \'current7d\' (last\r\n      // 7 days), \'prev7d\' (the 7 days before), or \'older\'. RLS on attempts still\r\n      // governs via SECURITY INVOKER.\r\n      const { data: rollup, error } = await supabase.rpc(\'attempt_accuracy_rollup\', {\r\n        p_class_id: classId,\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      const { data: analytics, opportunities } = computeAnalyticsFromRollup(rollup ?? []);\r\n      setData(analytics);\r\n      setOpportunities(opportunities);\r\n      setLoading(false);\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      console.error(\'Error loading analytics:\', error);\r\n      setLoadError(\'Could not load analytics. Check your connection and try again.\');\r\n      setLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const startDrill = (type?: string, level?: number, count = 6) => {\r\n    const manifest = questionBank.getManifest();\r\n    const pts = Object.keys(manifest.byPT).map(Number);\r\n    navigate(\'/drill\', {\r\n      state: {\r\n        mode: \'type-drill\',\r\n        config: { qtypes: type ? [type] : [], difficulties: level ? [level] : [], pts, count },\r\n      },\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (loading) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background">\r\n        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">\r\n          <div className="w-8 h-8 border border-border border-t-foreground/30 rounded-full animate-spin" />\r\n          <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Loading analytics</p>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const hasData = data && Object.keys(data.accuracyByType).length > 0;\r\n  const sortedTypes = hasData ? Object.keys(data.accuracyByType).sort() : [];\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    // No header of its own: the classroom rail owns the way back, sign out and\r\n    // the theme toggle. The page keeps its own h1 — the rail is navigation, not\r\n    // the document\'s heading, and stripping the header left the page untitled.\r\n    <div className="bg-background">\r\n      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-16">\r\n        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight mb-10">Analytics</h1>\r\n\r\n        {/* Tag breakdowns. Self-contained: reads the student\'s own attempts and\r\n            the question bank, so it renders regardless of the rollup below. */}\r\n        <div className="mb-10">\r\n          <BreakdownPanel />\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {loadError ? (\r\n          <div className="rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-sm p-12 text-center">\r\n            <p className="text-foreground text-sm font-medium">{loadError}</p>\r\n            <Button\r\n              variant="outline"\r\n              size="sm"\r\n              className="mt-4"\r\n              onClick={() => loadAnalytics()}\r\n            >\r\n              Retry\r\n            </Button>\r\n          </div>\r\n        ) : !hasData ? (\r\n          <div className="rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-sm p-12 text-center">\r\n            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">No data yet for this period</p>\r\n            <p className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground/70 mt-2">Complete some drills to see your analytics</p>\r\n          </div>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <>\r\n            {/* Top Opportunities */}\r\n            <div\r\n              ref={opportunitiesRef.ref}\r\n              className={cn(\r\n                \'mb-12 transition-all duration-700\',\r\n                opportunitiesRef.isVisible ? \'opacity-100 translate-y-0\' : \'opacity-0 translate-y-8\'\r\n              )}\r\n            >\r\n              <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground font-medium mb-5">\r\n                Top Opportunities\r\n              </p>\r\n              {opportunities.length === 0 ? (\r\n                <div className="rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-sm p-8 text-center">\r\n                  <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Great work — no significant improvement areas detected.</p>\r\n                </div>\r\n              ) : (\r\n                <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">\r\n                  {opportunities.map((opp, idx) => (\r\n                    <button\r\n                      key={idx}\r\n                      className={cn(\r\n                        \'group rounded-xl bg-card border border-border shadow-sm p-6\',\r\n                        \'hover:bg-accent hover:border-border\',\r\n                        \'transition-all duration-200 text-left focus:outline-none\',\r\n                      )}\r\n                      onClick={() => startDrill(opp.type, opp.level)}\r\n                    >\r\n                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center">\r\n                        <div className="relative w-28 h-28 mb-4">\r\n                          <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className="transform -rotate-90 w-full h-full">\r\n                            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke={svgTrack} strokeWidth="8" />\r\n                            <circle\r\n                              cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none"\r\n                              stroke={svgArc} strokeWidth="8"\r\n                              strokeDasharray={`${(opp.currentAccuracy / 100) * 251.2} 251.2`}\r\n                              strokeLinecap="round"\r\n                            />\r\n                          </svg>\r\n                          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">\r\n                            <div className="text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground tabular-nums">{opp.impact}</div>\r\n                            <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-[0.12em]">Impact</div>\r\n                          </div>\r\n                        </div>\r\n                        <div className="text-center">\r\n                          <div className="text-[13px] font-medium text-foreground/80">{opp.type} · L{opp.level}</div>\r\n                          <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">\r\n                            {Math.round(opp.currentAccuracy)}% accuracy · {opp.attempts} attempts\r\n                          </div>\r\n                          <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground/70 mt-1 group-hover:text-muted-foreground transition-colors">\r\n                            Tap to drill →\r\n                          </div>\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\n/**\r\n * Drill History — every question the student has ever attempted, across every\r\n * surface: drills, bootcamps, Reading Comp, Abstract Reasoning, Causation\r\n * Station. Two views over the same data:\r\n *\r\n *   Timeline    — an ordered, day-grouped list of attempts (newest first),\r\n *                 with the attempt number for that question ("Attempt #3").\r\n *   By question — a per-question rollup: attempts, accuracy, average and\r\n *                 total time, and the latest result.\r\n *\r\n * Data comes from the `drill_history` security-invoker view, so the student\r\n * only ever sees their own rows.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport {\r\n  SOURCE_LABELS,\r\n  attemptNumbers,\r\n  fetchDrillHistory,\r\n  formatDurationMs,\r\n  rollupByQuestion,\r\n  type DrillHistoryRow,\r\n  type DrillHistorySource,\r\n} from \'@/lib/drillHistory\';\r\nimport { fetchSectionHistory, type SectionHistoryRow } from \'@/lib/sectionHistory\';\r\nimport SectionHistoryList from \'@/components/history/SectionHistoryList\';\r\nimport { Check, ListFilter, Loader2, Minus, X } from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\n\r\ntype ViewMode = \'timeline\' | \'by-question\' | \'sections\';\r\n\r\nconst VIEW_LABELS: Record<ViewMode, string> = {\r\n  timeline: \'Timeline\',\r\n  \'by-question\': \'By question\',\r\n  sections: \'Sections\',\r\n};\r\ntype ResultFilter = \'all\' | \'correct\' | \'missed\';\r\ntype SourceFilter = \'all\' | DrillHistorySource;\r\n\r\nconst SOURCE_ORDER: DrillHistorySource[] = [\'lr\', \'intro-lr\', \'rc\', \'abstract\', \'causation\'];\r\n\r\nfunction dayLabel(iso: string): string {\r\n  const d = new Date(iso);\r\n  const today = new Date();\r\n  const yesterday = new Date();\r\n  yesterday.setDate(today.getDate() - 1);\r\n  const same = (a: Date, b: Date) => a.toDateString() === b.toDateString();\r\n  if (same(d, today)) return \'Today\';\r\n  if (same(d, yesterday)) return \'Yesterday\';\r\n  return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: \'short\', month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\', year: \'numeric\' });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction timeLabel(iso: string): string {\r\n  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: \'numeric\', minute: \'2-digit\' });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction ResultMark({ correct }: { correct: boolean | null }) {\r\n  if (correct === null) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <span\r\n        className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted text-muted-foreground"\r\n        title="Ungraded work"\r\n      >\r\n        <Minus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />\r\n      </span>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  return correct ? (\r\n    <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-success/15 text-success" title="Correct">\r\n      <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />\r\n    </span>\r\n  ) : (\r\n    <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-destructive/15 text-destructive" title="Missed">\r\n      <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />\r\n    </span>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction StatCard({ label, value, sub }: { label: string; value: string; sub?: string }) {\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4">\r\n      <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">{label}</p>\r\n      <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground">{value}</p>\r\n      {sub && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{sub}</p>}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst DrillHistory: React.FC = () => {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [rows, setRows] = useState<DrillHistoryRow[] | null>(null);\r\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const [view, setView] = useState<ViewMode>(\'timeline\');\r\n  const [sourceFilter, setSourceFilter] = useState<SourceFilter>(\'all\');\r\n  const [resultFilter, setResultFilter] = useState<ResultFilter>(\'all\');\r\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState(\'\');\r\n  const [sections, setSections] = useState<SectionHistoryRow[] | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  // Sections load alongside the attempts rather than on tab entry: the count\r\n  // belongs on the tab, and a student who lands here should not watch a second\r\n  // spinner to find out whether they have sat any.\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!user?.id) return;\r\n    let cancelled = false;\r\n    fetchSectionHistory(user.id)\r\n      .then((data) => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setSections(data);\r\n      })\r\n      .catch(() => {\r\n        // A section-history failure must not take down the attempts view, which\r\n        // is the page\'s main job. An empty list is the honest fallback.\r\n        if (!cancelled) setSections([]);\r\n      });\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n  }, [user?.id]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!user?.id) return;\r\n    let cancelled = false;\r\n    fetchDrillHistory(user.id)\r\n      .then((data) => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setRows(data);\r\n      })\r\n      .catch((e) => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : \'Could not load drill history\');\r\n      });\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n  }, [user?.id]);\r\n\r\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!rows) return [];\r\n    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    return rows.filter((row) => {\r\n      if (sourceFilter !== \'all\' && row.source !== sourceFilter) return false;\r\n      if (resultFilter === \'correct\' && row.correct !== true) return false;\r\n      if (resultFilter === \'missed\' && row.correct !== false) return false;\r\n      if (\r\n        q &&\r\n        !row.question_ref.toLowerCase().includes(q) &&\r\n        !(row.detail ?? \'\').toLowerCase().includes(q) &&\r\n        !(row.mode ?? \'\').toLowerCase().includes(q)\r\n      )\r\n        return false;\r\n      return true;\r\n    });\r\n  }, [rows, sourceFilter, resultFilter, search]);\r\n\r\n  const numbers = useMemo(() => (rows ? attemptNumbers(rows) : new Map()), [rows]);\r\n  const rollups = useMemo(() => rollupByQuestion(filtered), [filtered]);\r\n\r\n  const stats = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!rows) return null;\r\n    const graded = rows.filter((r) => r.correct !== null);\r\n    const correct = graded.filter((r) => r.correct).length;\r\n    const totalTime = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.time_ms ?? 0), 0);\r\n    const unique = new Set(rows.map((r) => `${r.source}|${r.question_key}`)).size;\r\n    return {\r\n      attempts: rows.length,\r\n      accuracy: graded.length > 0 ? Math.round((correct / graded.length) * 100) : null,\r\n      totalTime,\r\n      unique,\r\n    };\r\n  }, [rows]);\r\n\r\n  const dayGroups = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const groups: { label: string; rows: DrillHistoryRow[] }[] = [];\r\n    for (const row of filtered) {\r\n      const label = dayLabel(row.attempted_at);\r\n      const last = groups[groups.length - 1];\r\n      if (last && last.label === label) last.rows.push(row);\r\n      else groups.push({ label, rows: [row] });\r\n    }\r\n    return groups;\r\n  }, [filtered]);\r\n\r\n  const presentSources = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!rows) return [];\r\n    const set = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.source));\r\n    return SOURCE_ORDER.filter((s) => set.has(s));\r\n  }, [rows]);\r\n\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="flex min-h-full items-center justify-center bg-background p-8">\r\n        <div className="max-w-md rounded-xl border border-destructive/30 bg-card p-6 text-center">\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q3\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";\r\nimport { useClassId } from "@/hooks/useClassId";\r\nimport {\r\n  getWrongAnswerSmartRows,\r\n  type WrongAnswerSmartRow,\r\n} from "@/lib/journalService";\r\n\r\nexport function journalEntriesQueryKey(classId: string) {\r\n  return ["journal", "entries", classId] as const;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useJournalEntries(enabled = true) {\r\n  const { classId, loading: classLoading } = useClassId();\r\n  const query = useQuery<WrongAnswerSmartRow[]>({\r\n    queryKey: journalEntriesQueryKey(classId),\r\n    enabled: enabled && !!classId,\r\n    staleTime: 60_000,\r\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,\r\n    queryFn: () => getWrongAnswerSmartRows(classId),\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    ...query,\r\n    classId,\r\n    isLoading: classLoading || query.isLoading,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useInvalidateJournalEntries() {\r\n  const { classId } = useClassId();\r\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\r\n  return () =>\r\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({\r\n      queryKey: journalEntriesQueryKey(classId),\r\n    });\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q4\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { listFlagged, type FlaggedQuestionRow } from \'@/lib/flaggedService\';\r\n\r\nexport function flaggedQueryKey(userId: string | undefined) {\r\n  return [\'flagged-questions\', userId] as const;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The student\'s flagged questions, newest first.\r\n *\r\n * Callers must keep the three states distinct: only a query that SUCCEEDED\r\n * with zero rows may render the empty state. A failed fetch has to show the\r\n * error and a retry, never a calm "nothing flagged".\r\n */\r\nexport function useFlagged() {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  return useQuery<FlaggedQuestionRow[]>({\r\n    queryKey: flaggedQueryKey(user?.id),\r\n    enabled: !!user,\r\n    staleTime: 60_000,\r\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,\r\n    queryFn: () => listFlagged(user!.id),\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Write helpers need to invalidate the same key the list reads. */\r\nexport function useInvalidateFlagged() {\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\r\n  return () =>\r\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: flaggedQueryKey(user?.id) });\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q5\n/**\r\n * One practice test, in progress.\r\n *\r\n * This page is the invigilator, not the exam. It says which section is next,\r\n * starts it, runs the break, reads each leg\'s result back out of the tables the\r\n * runners write, and scores the sitting at the end. Every actual question is\r\n * answered somewhere else, in the same runner the student uses outside a test.\r\n *\r\n * The result readback is deliberately conservative: a leg with no matching row\r\n * is reported as unfinished rather than scored as zero. A zero here would read\r\n * on the score report as "answered everything wrong", which is a different and\r\n * much more discouraging claim than "you walked away from section 2".\r\n */\r\nimport * as React from "react";\r\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";\r\nimport { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport {\r\n  formatPracticeTestClock,\r\n  legLabel,\r\n  loadPracticeTestRun,\r\n  practiceTestPaceLabel,\r\n  practiceTestRunSectionSeconds,\r\n  practiceTestRaw,\r\n  practiceTestScaled,\r\n  savePracticeTestRun,\r\n  PRACTICE_TEST_BREAK_SECONDS,\r\n  type PracticeTestRun as Run,\r\n} from "@/lib/practiceTest";\r\nimport { lrLegLaunch, rcLegHref, readLegResult } from "@/lib/practiceTestRunner";\r\nimport "./testing-centre.css";\r\n\r\nfunction mmss(seconds: number) {\r\n  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));\r\n  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * What a ticking clock says out loud.\r\n *\r\n * The digits change twice a second, and a live region wired straight to them\r\n * would talk over itself for ten minutes without pause. Coarse steps instead:\r\n * whole minutes while minutes remain, then ten-second steps inside the last\r\n * one. The string changes only when the step does, so the region speaks only\r\n * when there is something new to say.\r\n */\r\nfunction breakAnnouncement(remaining: number): string {\r\n  const seconds = Math.ceil(remaining);\r\n  if (seconds <= 0) return "The break is over. Continue when you are ready.";\r\n  if (seconds <= 60) return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 10) * 10} seconds of the break left.`;\r\n  const minutes = Math.ceil(seconds / 60);\r\n  return `${minutes} ${minutes === 1 ? "minute" : "minutes"} of the break left.`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The break, on its own clock. Skippable — it is a break, not a gate. */\r\nfunction BreakScreen({ run, onDone }: { run: Run; onDone: () => void }) {\r\n  const startedAt = run.breakStartedAt ? Date.parse(run.breakStartedAt) : Date.now();\r\n  const [remaining, setRemaining] = React.useState(() =>\r\n    Math.max(0, PRACTICE_TEST_BREAK_SECONDS - (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000),\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    // Nothing left to count. The interval used to keep firing at 2Hz for as\r\n    // long as the student sat on a finished break.\r\n    if (remaining <= 0) return;\r\n    const id = window.setInterval(() => {\r\n      setRemaining(Math.max(0, PRACTICE_TEST_BREAK_SECONDS - (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000));\r\n    }, 500);\r\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\r\n  }, [startedAt, remaining]);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="testing-panel">\r\n      <h2 className="testing-heading">Break</h2>\r\n      <div className="testing-score">\r\n        {/* The digits are for the eye. Assistive tech gets the coarse\r\n            announcement instead of a number that changes 1,200 times. */}\r\n        <strong aria-hidden>{mmss(remaining)}</strong>\r\n        <span aria-hidden>break remaining</span>\r\n        <span className="sr-only" role="timer" aria-live="polite">\r\n          {breakAnnouncement(remaining)}\r\n        </span>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <p className="testing-note">\r\n        Stand up, get water, look at something further away than a screen. The\r\n        real test gives you this, and taking it is part of practising the\r\n        sitting rather than just the questions.\r\n      </p>\r\n      <div className="testing-actions">\r\n        <Button onClick={onDone}>\r\n          {remaining > 0 ? "Skip the break and continue" : "Continue"}\r\n        </Button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction ScoreReport({ run, onExit }: { run: Run; onExit: () => void }) {\r\n  const { correct, total } = practiceTestRaw(run);\r\n  const scaled = practiceTestScaled(run);\r\n  const unfinished = run.legs.filter((l) => !l.completedAt);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="testing-panel">\r\n      <h2 className="testing-heading">Score report</h2>\r\n      <div className="testing-score">\r\n        <strong>{scaled}</strong>\r\n        <span>\r\n          estimated score · {correct} of {total} correct\r\n        </span>\r\n      </div>\r\n      {/* Not fine print. This sentence is the difference between a number a\r\n          student can act on and one they will quote at themselves for a week,\r\n          and it sat at 12.5px and 2.10:1 under a 56px figure. */}\r\n      <p className="testing-note testing-note-lead">\r\n        Estimated from a typical modern LSAT curve, not PrepTest {run.pt}\'s own\r\n        conversion table. Treat it as a band, not a number.\r\n      </p>\r\n\r\n      <div className="testing-shape">\r\n        {run.legs.map((leg, i) => (\r\n          <div\r\n            className="testing-shape-row"\r\n            data-state={leg.completedAt ? "done" : undefined}\r\n            key={`${leg.kind}-${leg.section}`}\r\n          >\r\n            <b>Section {i + 1}</b>\r\n            <span>{legLabel(leg)}</span>\r\n            <em>\r\n              {leg.completedAt\r\n                ? `${leg.correct ?? 0}/${leg.questionCount}`\r\n                : "not finished"}\r\n            </em>\r\n          </div>\r\n        ))}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {unfinished.length > 0 && (\r\n        <p className="testing-note">\r\n          {unfinished.length === 1 ? "One section was" : `${unfinished.length} sections were`}{" "}\r\n          left unfinished, so those questions count against the raw total. The\r\n          estimate is lower than a completed sitting would give you.\r\n        </p>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n      <div className="testing-actions">\r\n        <Button onClick={onExit}>Back to the Testing Centre</Button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default function PracticeTestRunPage() {\r\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [run, setRun] = React.useState<Run | null | undefined>(undefined);\r\n  const [checking, setChecking] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const [noResult, setNoResult] = React.useState(false);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    setRun(runId ? loadPracticeTestRun(runId) : null);\r\n  }, [runId]);\r\n\r\n  const commit = React.useCallback((next: Run) => {\r\n    savePracticeTestRun(next);\r\n    setRun(next);\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "Q0\n/**\r\n * Unified drill history — reads `public.drill_history`, the security-invoker\r\n * view that unions every per-question attempt source on the platform:\r\n *\r\n *   lr         → attempts (Smart Drill, type drills, guided drills, sections)\r\n *   intro-lr   → intro_lr_attempts (Main Conclusion bootcamp)\r\n *   rc         → rc_attempts (Reading Comprehension)\r\n *   abstract   → abstract_drill_attempts (Abstract Reasoning)\r\n *   causation  → cs_attempts (Causation Station)\r\n *\r\n * The view is not in the generated Supabase types yet, so rows are cast at\r\n * the call site — the established pattern in this repo for objects ahead of\r\n * a types regeneration.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\r\n\r\nexport type DrillHistorySource = 'lr' | 'intro-lr' | 'rc' | 'abstract' | 'causation';\r\n\r\nexport const SOURCE_LABELS: Record<DrillHistorySource, string> = {\r\n  lr: 'Drills',\r\n  'intro-lr': 'Intro to LR',\r\n  rc: 'Reading',\r\n  abstract: 'Abstract',\r\n  causation: 'Causation Station',\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport interface DrillHistoryRow {\r\n  user_id: string;\r\n  source: DrillHistorySource;\r\n  question_key: string;\r\n  question_ref: string;\r\n  detail: string | null;\r\n  correct: boolean | null;\r\n  time_ms: number | null;\r\n  selected_answer: string | null;\r\n  mode: string | null;\r\n  attempted_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface QuestionRollup {\r\n  source: DrillHistorySource;\r\n  questionKey: string;\r\n  questionRef: string;\r\n  detail: string | null;\r\n  attempts: number;\r\n  gradedAttempts: number;\r\n  correctCount: number;\r\n  /** null when every attempt is ungraded (free-response work). */\r\n  accuracy: number | null;\r\n  avgTimeMs: number | null;\r\n  totalTimeMs: number;\r\n  lastCorrect: boolean | null;\r\n  lastAttemptedAt: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 1000;\r\n\r\n/** Fetches the student's full attempt history, oldest data included. */\r\nexport async function fetchDrillHistory(userId: string): Promise<DrillHistoryRow[]> {\r\n  const rows: DrillHistoryRow[] = [];\r\n  let from = 0;\r\n  // Page through the whole history — a student with years of work should\r\n  // still see every attempt, not the newest thousand.\r\n  for (;;) {\r\n    const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n      .from('drill_history' as never)\r\n      .select('*')\r\n      .eq('user_id', userId)\r\n      .order('attempted_at', { ascending: false })\r\n      .range(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);\r\n    if (error) throw new Error(error.message ?? 'Could not load drill history');\r\n    const batch = (data ?? []) as unknown as DrillHistoryRow[];\r\n    rows.push(...batch);\r\n    if (batch.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;\r\n    from += PAGE_SIZE;\r\n  }\r\n  return rows;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Per-question rollup over a set of history rows (already filtered). */\r\nexport function rollupByQuestion(rows: DrillHistoryRow[]): QuestionRollup[] {\r\n  const byKey = new Map<string, QuestionRollup & { _timeCount: number }>();\r\n  for (const row of rows) {\r\n    const key = `${row.source}|${row.question_key}`;\r\n    let agg = byKey.get(key);\r\n    if (!agg) {\r\n      agg = {\r\n        source: row.source,\r\n        questionKey: row.question_key,\r\n        questionRef: row.question_ref,\r\n        detail: row.detail,\r\n        attempts: 0,\r\n        gradedAttempts: 0,\r\n        correctCount: 0,\r\n        accuracy: null,\r\n        avgTimeMs: null,\r\n        totalTimeMs: 0,\r\n        lastCorrect: null,\r\n        lastAttemptedAt: row.attempted_at,\r\n        _timeCount: 0,\r\n      };\r\n      byKey.set(key, agg);\r\n    }\r\n    agg.attempts += 1;\r\n    if (row.correct !== null) {\r\n      agg.gradedAttempts += 1;\r\n      if (row.correct) agg.correctCount += 1;\r\n    }\r\n    if (row.time_ms != null) {\r\n      agg.totalTimeMs += row.time_ms;\r\n      agg._timeCount += 1;\r\n    }\r\n    // rows arrive newest-first from fetchDrillHistory, so the first row seen\r\n    // for a question is its latest take.\r\n    if (agg.attempts === 1 || row.attempted_at > agg.lastAttemptedAt) {\r\n      agg.lastAttemptedAt = row.attempted_at;\r\n      agg.lastCorrect = row.correct;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  const result: QuestionRollup[] = [];\r\n  for (const agg of byKey.values()) {\r\n    agg.accuracy = agg.gradedAttempts > 0 ? agg.correctCount / agg.gradedAttempts : null;\r\n    agg.avgTimeMs = agg._timeCount > 0 ? Math.round(agg.totalTimeMs / agg._timeCount) : null;\r\n    const { _timeCount, ...rest } = agg;\r\n    void _timeCount;\r\n    result.push(rest);\r\n  }\r\n  result.sort((a, b) => b.lastAttemptedAt.localeCompare(a.lastAttemptedAt));\r\n  return result;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Attempt number of each row within its question's history (1 = first ever).\r\n * Input must be the full fetched set; rows can be in any order.\r\n */\r\nexport function attemptNumbers(rows: DrillHistoryRow[]): Map<DrillHistoryRow, number> {\r\n  const byKey = new Map<string, DrillHistoryRow[]>();\r\n  for (const row of rows) {\r\n    const key = `${row.source}|${row.question_key}`;\r\n    const list = byKey.get(key);\r\n    if (list) list.push(row);\r\n    else byKey.set(key, [row]);\r\n  }\r\n  const numbers = new Map<DrillHistoryRow, number>();\r\n  for (const list of byKey.values()) {\r\n    list.sort((a, b) => a.attempted_at.localeCompare(b.attempted_at));\r\n    list.forEach((row, i) => numbers.set(row, i + 1));\r\n  }\r\n  return numbers;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function formatDurationMs(ms: number | null | undefined): string {\r\n  if (ms == null) return '—';\r\n  const totalSeconds = Math.round(ms / 1000);\r\n  if (totalSeconds < 60) return `${totalSeconds}s`;\r\n  const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);\r\n  const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;\r\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;\r\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\r\n  const restMinutes = minutes % 60;\r\n  return `${hours}h ${restMinutes}m`;\r\n}\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q1\n/**\r\n * journalService — data layer for the Notion-style Journal workspace.\r\n *\r\n * All journal_* tables are USER-scoped (RLS: auth.uid() = user_id). This is a\r\n * DIFFERENT id space from `wrong_answer_journal`, which is CLASS-scoped\r\n * (class_id). Never cross the streams: never pass a class_id to a journal_*\r\n * query, and never pass a user_id to a wrong_answer_journal query. The only\r\n * bridge is getWrongAnswerSmartRows(), which reads the class-scoped feed and\r\n * maps it into the read-only "smart" database surface.\r\n *\r\n * Errors are never swallowed: every call throws on failure so callers can\r\n * surface a toast. (See repo rule: no silent console.error.)\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport type { Json } from \'@/integrations/supabase/types\';\r\nimport { getWAJEntries, type WAJEntry } from \'@/lib/wajService\';\r\nimport { getNotesForClass, type WAJNote, type ReviewStatus } from \'@/lib/wajNotesService\';\r\n\r\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n// Types\r\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n\r\nexport type JournalPageType = \'doc\' | \'database\';\r\nexport type JournalColumnType =\r\n  | \'text\'\r\n  | \'number\'\r\n  | \'select\'\r\n  | \'multi_select\'\r\n  | \'date\'\r\n  | \'checkbox\'\r\n  | \'url\';\r\nexport type JournalViewKind = \'table\' | \'board\' | \'gallery\';\r\n\r\n/** Minimal TipTap/ProseMirror document shape. Stored verbatim in journal_pages.body. */\r\nexport interface TiptapDoc {\r\n  type: \'doc\';\r\n  content?: unknown[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport const EMPTY_DOC: TiptapDoc = { type: \'doc\', content: [] };\r\n\r\nexport interface JournalPage {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  user_id: string;\r\n  parent_page_id: string | null;\r\n  title: string;\r\n  icon: string | null;\r\n  type: JournalPageType;\r\n  body: TiptapDoc;\r\n  /** Row values for database rows, keyed by column id. Empty for docs/databases. */\r\n  properties: Record<string, unknown>;\r\n  sort_order: number;\r\n  is_archived: boolean;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  updated_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface JournalColumn {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  name: string;\r\n  type: JournalColumnType;\r\n  /** For select / multi_select. */\r\n  options?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface JournalDatabase {\r\n  page_id: string;\r\n  user_id: string;\r\n  columns: JournalColumn[];\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  updated_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface JournalViewConfig {\r\n  /** Column ids in display order; absent → all columns. */\r\n  visibleColumns?: string[];\r\n  /** Column id to group by (board view). */\r\n  groupBy?: string;\r\n  /** Column id used as the gallery/board card cover. */\r\n  coverColumn?: string;\r\n  sort?: { columnId: string; direction: \'asc\' | \'desc\' };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface JournalView {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  database_id: string;\r\n  user_id: string;\r\n  name: string;\r\n  kind: JournalViewKind;\r\n  config: JournalViewConfig;\r\n  sort_order: number;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  updated_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n// Row mappers (DB row → typed domain object)\r\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n\r\nfunction toPage(row: Record<string, unknown>): JournalPage {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: row.id as string,\r\n    user_id: row.user_id as string,\r\n    parent_page_id: (row.parent_page_id as string | null) ?? null,\r\n    title: (row.title as string) ?? \'Untitled\',\r\n    icon: (row.icon as string | null) ?? null,\r\n    type: (row.type as JournalPageType) ?? \'doc\',\r\n    body: (row.body as TiptapDoc) ?? EMPTY_DOC,\r\n    properties: (row.properties as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},\r\n    sort_order: (row.sort_order as number) ?? 0,\r\n    is_archived: !!row.is_archived,\r\n    created_at: row.created_at as string,\r\n    updated_at: row.updated_at as string,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction toDatabase(row: Record<string, unknown>): JournalDatabase {\r\n  return {\r\n    page_id: row.page_id as string,\r\n    user_id: row.user_id as string,\r\n    columns: (row.columns as JournalColumn[]) ?? [],\r\n    created_at: row.created_at as string,\r\n    updated_at: row.updated_at as string,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction toView(row: Record<string, unknown>): JournalView {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: row.id as string,\r\n    database_id: row.database_id as string,\r\n    user_id: row.user_id as string,\r\n    name: (row.name as string) ?? \'Table\',\r\n    kind: (row.kind as JournalViewKind) ?? \'table\',\r\n    config: (row.config as JournalViewConfig) ?? {},\r\n    sort_order: (row.sort_order as number) ?? 0,\r\n    created_at: row.created_at as string,\r\n    updated_at: row.updated_at as string,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Resolve the current authenticated user id, required for inserts (RLS). */\r\nasync function requireUserId(): Promise<string> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  const uid = data.user?.id;\r\n  if (!uid) throw new Error(\'Not authenticated\');\r\n  return uid;\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\n/**\r\n * flaggedService — the service layer `flagged_questions` never had.\r\n *\r\n * A flag is a come-back-to-this marker the student raises mid-question, from\r\n * Drill or either Blind Review flow. It is NOT a record of a missed question;\r\n * that is the Wrong Answer Journal (`wrong_answer_journal`), a different table\r\n * with a different meaning.\r\n *\r\n * Before this, the table was queried inline in five components. Anything new\r\n * should come through here.\r\n */\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport type { Database } from \'@/integrations/supabase/types\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Derived from the generated schema rather than hand-written. The old page\r\n * declared its own local interface listing only six of the eleven columns,\r\n * which is how `class_id`, `level` and `qtype` went missing.\r\n */\r\nexport type FlaggedQuestionRow =\r\n  Database[\'public\'][\'Tables\'][\'flagged_questions\'][\'Row\'];\r\n\r\n/** The shape needed to put a flag back after an accidental unflag. */\r\nexport type FlagRestorePayload =\r\n  Database[\'public\'][\'Tables\'][\'flagged_questions\'][\'Insert\'];\r\n\r\nexport function flagLabel(row: Pick<FlaggedQuestionRow, \'pt\' | \'section\' | \'qnum\'>) {\r\n  return `PT${row.pt}-S${row.section}-Q${row.qnum}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function listFlagged(userId: string): Promise<FlaggedQuestionRow[]> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'flagged_questions\')\r\n    .select(\'*\')\r\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\r\n    .order(\'flagged_at\', { ascending: false });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return (data ?? []) as FlaggedQuestionRow[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function updateFlagNote(id: string, note: string): Promise<void> {\r\n  // `.select()` matters: an RLS-blocked UPDATE returns success with zero rows\r\n  // rather than an error, so without this the UI would report "saved" for a\r\n  // write that never happened.\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'flagged_questions\')\r\n    .update({ note: note.trim() ? note : null })\r\n    .eq(\'id\', id)\r\n    .select(\'id\');\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  if (!data || data.length === 0) {\r\n    throw new Error(\'That note was not saved. You may not have permission to edit this flag.\');\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function unflag(id: string): Promise<void> {\r\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\'flagged_questions\').delete().eq(\'id\', id);\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Puts an unflagged question back. The row gets a fresh id (the original is\r\n * gone) but `flagged_at` is preserved, so undo does not silently reorder the\r\n * list under the student.\r\n */\r\nexport async function restoreFlag(\r\n  payload: FlagRestorePayload,\r\n): Promise<FlaggedQuestionRow> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from(\'flagged_questions\')\r\n    .insert(payload)\r\n    .select()\r\n    .single();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data as FlaggedQuestionRow;\r\n}\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': localStorage.getItem(storeKey(id));\r\n    if (!raw) return null;\r\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as PracticeTestRun;\r\n    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.legs)) return null;\r\n    return parsed;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function listPracticeTestRuns(): PracticeTestRun[] {\r\n  const runs: PracticeTestRun[] = [];\r\n  try {\r\n    for (let i = toast.error(\r\n            error instanceof Error ? error.message : \'Could not update the schedule\',\r\n          ),\r\n      },\r\n    );\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const applyLevel = (next: StudentLevel | \'border-emerald-700/25 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-800\'\r\n                    : \'border-black/15 text-black/55 hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black\',\r\n                )}\r\n              >\r\n                {LEVEL_LABELS[option]}\r\n                <span className="ml-1.5 font-normal text-black/40">\r\n                  {describeBand(levelBand(option)).replace(\'difficulty \', \'\')}\r\n                </span>\r\n              </button>\r\n            );\r\n          })}\r\n          {level && (\r\n            <button\r\n              type="button"\r\n              disabled={setLevel.isPending}\r\n              onClick={() => applyLevel(null)}\r\n              className="px-1.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/40 underline-offset-2 transition hover:text-black hover:underline disabled:opacity-50"\r\n            >\r\n              Clear\r\n            </button>\r\n          )}\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {!level && (\r\n          <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs text-black/40">Not set.</p>\r\n        )}\r\n      </section>\r\n\r\n      <section className="border border-black/10 bg-white/20 p-4">\r\n        <h2 className="text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-black/45">\r\n          Schedule\r\n        </h2>\r\n        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">\r\n          The shape of this student\'s week: which days they work, where the timed day and the\r\n          rest day fall. The curriculum fills the drill slot on each skill day.\r\n        </p>\r\n\r\n        {schedules.length === 0 ? (\r\n          <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs text-black/40">\r\n            {schedulesLoading ? \'Loading…\' : \'No schedules yet.\'}\r\n          </p>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <>\r\n            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">\r\n              {schedules.map((option) => {\r\n                const on = schedule?.id === option.id;\r\n                return (\r\n                  <button\r\n                    key={option.id}\r\n                    type="button"\r\n                    disabled={setSchedule.isPending}\r\n                    onClick={() => applySchedule(on ? null : option.id)}\r\n                    aria-pressed={on}\r\n                    className={cn(\r\n                      \'border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition disabled:opacity-50\',\r\n                      on\r\n                        ? \'border-emerald-700/25 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-800\'\r\n                        : \'border-black/15 text-black/55 hover:bg-white/25 hover:text-black\',\r\n                    )}\r\n                  >\r\n                    {option.title}\r\n                    <span className="ml-1.5 font-normal text-black/40">\r\n                      {templateStudyDays(option.days).length} days\r\n                    </span>\r\n                  </button>\r\n                );\r\n              })}\r\n              {schedule && (\r\n                <button\r\n                  type="button"\r\n                  disabled={setSchedule.isPending}\r\n                  onClick={() => applySchedule(null)}\r\n                  className="px-1.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black/40 underline-offset-2 transition hover:text-black hover:underline disabled:opacity-50"\r\n                >\r\n                  Clear\r\n                </button>\r\n              )}\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n            {schedule ? (\r\n              <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs text-black/45">{schedule.description}</p>\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs text-black/40">\r\n                Not set; this student is on the default week.\r\n              </p>\r\n            )}\r\n          </>\r\n        )}\r\n      </section>\r\n\r\n      <section className="border border-black/10 bg-white/20 p-4">\r\n        <h2 className="text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.04em] text-black/45">Access</h2>\r\n        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-black/45">\r\n          Bootcamp access is controlled by assignment, not here. Assign or archive a bootcamp from\r\n          the Library to add or remove it from this student\'s path.\r\n        </p>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">\r\n          {CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.map((key) => {\r\n            const on = readFlag(student as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, key);\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q3\nimport { useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\r\nimport {\r\n  GraduationCap,\r\n  CheckCircle2,\r\n  Circle,\r\n  PlayCircle,\r\n  Sparkles,\r\n  FileText,\r\n  Trash2,\r\n  Megaphone,\r\n  BookOpen,\r\n} from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { toast } from \'sonner\';\r\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\r\nimport { formatRelativeShort } from \'@/lib/time\';\r\nimport {\r\n  useAdminTAAssignmentsForStudent,\r\n  type StudentTAAssignment,\r\n} from \'@/hooks/useStudentAssignments\';\r\nimport { useStudentClassroomItems, type ClassroomItem } from \'@/hooks/useClassroomItems\';\r\nimport {\r\n  AlertDialog,\r\n  AlertDialogAction,\r\n  AlertDialogCancel,\r\n  AlertDialogContent,\r\n  AlertDialogDescription,\r\n  AlertDialogFooter,\r\n  AlertDialogHeader,\r\n  AlertDialogTitle,\r\n} from \'@/components/ui/alert-dialog\';\r\nimport { Button } from \'@/components/ui/button\';\r\n\r\ninterface Props {\r\n  studentId: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\ninterface HomeworkRow {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  set_title: string;\r\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\r\n  score: number | null;\r\n  created_at: string;\r\n  completed_at: string | null;\r\n  question_qids: string[] | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst HW_STATUS_ICON = {\r\n  assigned: Circle,\r\n  in_progress: PlayCircle,\r\n  completed: CheckCircle2,\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst TA_STATUS_ICON = {\r\n  assigned: Circle,\r\n  viewed: PlayCircle,\r\n  completed: CheckCircle2,\r\n};\r\n\r\ntype PendingDelete =\r\n  | { kind: \'homework\'; id: string; title: string }\r\n  | { kind: \'ta\'; id: string; title: string }\r\n  | { kind: \'classroom\'; id: string; title: string };\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Center-panel "Assignments" tab. Real homework_assignments + ta_assignments\r\n * + classroom_items (posts/bootcamps) for the selected student. Admins can\r\n * unassign / delete each row, which removes it from the student\'s view.\r\n */\r\nexport default function HubAssignments({ studentId }: Props) {\r\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\r\n  const [pending, setPending] = useState<PendingDelete | null>(null);\r\n  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const { data: homework, isLoading: hwLoading } = useQuery<HomeworkRow[]>({\r\n    queryKey: [\'hub-homework\', studentId],\r\n    enabled: !!studentId,\r\n    queryFn: async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from(\'homework_assignments\')\r\n        .select(\'id, set_title, status, score, created_at, completed_at, question_qids\')\r\n        .eq(\'student_id\', studentId)\r\n        .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: false });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return (data ?? []) as HomeworkRow[];\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  const { assignments: taAssignments, loading: taLoading, refresh: refreshTA } =\r\n    useAdminTAAssignmentsForStudent(studentId);\r\n\r\n  const { data: classroomItems, isLoading: ciLoading } =\r\n    useStudentClassroomItems(studentId);\r\n\r\n  const posts = (classroomItems ?? []).filter(\r\n    (i) => i.type !== \'drill\' && i.type !== \'homework\',\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const hasAny =\r\n    (homework?.length ?? 0) > 0 ||\r\n    taAssignments.length > 0 ||\r\n    posts.length > 0;\r\n\r\n  async function confirmDelete() {\r\n    if (!pending) return;\r\n    setDeleting(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      let error: { message: string } | null = null;\r\n      if (pending.kind === \'homework\') {\r\n        const r = await supabase\r\n          .from(\'homework_assignments\')\r\n          .delete()\r\n          .eq(\'id\', pending.id);\r\n        error = r.error;\r\n        if (!error) await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'hub-homework\', studentId] });\r\n      } else if (pending.kind === \'ta\') {\r\n        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n        const r = await supabase\r\n          .from(\'ta_assignments\')\r\n          .delete()\r\n          .eq(\'id\', pending.id);\r\n        error = r.error;\r\n        if (!error) await refreshTA();\r\n      } else {\r\n        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n        const r = await supabase\r\n          .from(\'classroom_items\')\r\n          .delete()\r\n          .eq(\'id\', pending.id);\r\n        error = r.error;\r\n        if (!error)\r\n          await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'classroom-items-admin\', studentId] });\r\n      }\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        console.error(\'[hub:assignments:delete] failed\', error);\r\n        toast.error(\'Could not unassign\', { description: error.message });\r\n      } else {\r\n        toast.success(\'Removed from student\');\r\n        setPending(null);\r\n      }\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setDeleting(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (!hwLoading && !taLoading && !ciLoading && !hasAny) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-center px-6 py-12">\r\n        <div className="max-w-sm space-y-2">\r\n          <GraduationCap className="w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground/70 mx-auto" />\r\n          <div className="text-sm font-medium">No assignments yet</div>\r\n          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">\r\n            Homework, bootcamps, and posts you assign will show up here so you\r\n            can edit or unassign them.\r\n          </p>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-6">\r\n      <Section\r\n        title="Homework"\r\n        icon={GraduationCap}\r\n        loading={hwLoading}\r\n        empty={!hwLoading && (homework?.length ?? 0) === 0}\r\n      >\r\n        {homework?.map((h) => {\r\n          const Icon = HW_STATUS_ICON[h.status];\r\n'}]
