## Tool: exec

const qs=["rg -n 'seven|Seven|destination|Student|My Work|Progress|Review|Teacher' brainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md -m 20","rg -n 'view.*student|teacher.*answer|saved|preview|mode' brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md -m 10"];const r=await Promise.all(qs.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os",login:false,max_output_tokens:4000})));r.forEach((x,i)=>text("Q"+i+"\n"+x.output))


## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "Q0\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:10:Joshua's 2026-09-28 screenshot shows a recognizable LSAT U Home: warm surfaces, a greeting, a prominent overdue assignment, quick actions, assigned work, a message from Joshua, and a seven-day strip. These are useful starting points. It also shows the cost of the current rail: Home, Drill, Practice Exams, Assigned work, Notes and recaps, Bootcamps, Activities, Review, Analytics, Admissions, History, Messages, and Schedule all compete at the same level. The Home hero and large assigned-work card leave little room to see the next study decision and its reason together.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:14:## One rail, with destinations students can predict\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:21:| Assigned work | My Work > To do / Completed | Each work row links to the right Practice, Activity, or test surface. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:22:| Notes and recaps | My Work > Notes & recaps | Notes and recaps retain their own record types and context. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:24:| Activities | Library > Activities | The assigned instance also appears in My Work. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:25:| Review, Wrong answers, Flagged | Review > To review / Wrong answers / Flagged | A single queue explains why each question is there. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:26:| Analytics and History | Progress > Overview / History / Topics | Counts disclose the included source and sample size. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:32:The seven primary rail destinations are Home, My Work, Practice, Review, Progress, Library, and Schedule. Settings and account controls stay with the profile. A student with restricted access still sees every tab; unavailable tabs show a lock and cannot open their pages. Existing child permission rules still apply to direct links. Existing deep links and saved rail preferences need migration as specified in the main game plan.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:34:## Student screen behavior\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:36:1. **Home answers what to do next.** The first recommendation states its source, why it is next, and a single Continue action. A message and a second assignment stay nearby. A short review-due line links directly into Review. The seven-day strip stays, but it does not crowd out the work.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:37:2. **My Work is the teacher relationship.** To do and Completed keep every issued assignment, even when Joshua assigns the same drill twice. Opening work does not make it In progress. Measurable practice moves to Completed only after its finish is saved. Notes & recaps includes Joshua's notes, session recaps, and the student's own notes with clear author and type labels. An unstarted assignment appears before any attempt exists.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:39:4. **Review is a task, then a record.** To review includes missed, flagged, skipped, and explicitly Joshua-selected questions or responses. Wrong answers and Flagged let students audit the underlying history. The view keeps the first answer, any blind answer, written diagnosis, explanation, and Joshua's note. For a missed answer, a short error explanation is required before Mark reviewed. That action clears the task but retains the flag and history; a new miss creates a new task.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:40:5. **Progress explains a next action.** Summary cards link to the underlying attempts and to a relevant review or drill. Empty data says there is not enough evidence, rather than displaying zero accuracy.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:41:6. **Library holds longer-lived material.** Lessons, Activities, Bootcamps, and Admissions live here. Students see Admissions locked while it is unfinished; Joshua/admins retain access. Assigned Activities also appear in My Work so the student can find them through either task or resource context.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:46:- The rail target is 248 to 256 px with seven full labels, one active state, and no second permanent navigation column. This is a prototype target to test at laptop widths.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:47:- The top bar carries search and Inbox. Search is a task shortcut; Inbox's unread count is persistent without making Messages an eighth primary destination.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:55:Joshua confirmed on 2026-09-28 that Practice is the student rail destination and Practice Exams is a distinct area inside it. This keeps the shorter rail without losing the [2026-09-26 Practice Exams decisions](2026-09-26-practice-exams-discovery.md). The first level is **Drills | Practice Exams**. Practice Exams then has **Overview | All Exams | Attempts** with links that reopen the same selected view.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:57:Overview starts with **Start a full PrepTest** and **Start a timed section**. Below them, **Unfinished exams and sections** shows both kinds of work with a clear Continue action. All Exams lists PrepTests; a detail page shows sections, start options, and that student's attempts. Attempts lists in-progress and completed full exams and timed sections, with Results for completed work. General question history remains in Progress.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:7:Student-facing companion: [2026-09-28 student experience design and clickable prototype](2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md). It maps every current student rail item from Joshua's screenshot into the proposed seven-page shell, including persistent Inbox and question search.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:11:The [route and saved-rail decision](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/157) sets stable paths for the seven destinations and local tabs, preserves old question and work links, and migrates saved rail choices once. Joshua has chosen to lock unfinished Admissions for students now while retaining Joshua/admin access; the [Admissions access ticket](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/164) tracks the live change.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:23:1. **The 2026-09-27 navigation decision wins:** My Work contains To do, Completed, and Notes & recaps. Review groups wrong and flagged work. Progress groups analytics and history. Notes and recaps remain distinct records. This supersedes the older draft's rail rename to “Notes,” while preserving its underlying recap type.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:24:2. **Keep the existing teacher desk.** Source inspection found four current teacher rooms, Today, Inbox, Library, and Insights. Library already has Bootcamps and Activities as separate URL-backed tabs. Improve these rooms and add a clear View student experience action. Do not rebuild the teacher rail as the earlier audit's speculative Students, Plans, Library, Schedule list.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:25:3. **Keep Practice Exams intact inside Practice.** Joshua confirmed on 2026-09-28 that the student rail can use Practice as one destination, with Drills and Practice Exams as distinct first-level areas. Practice Exams retains the previously approved Overview landing page with full PrepTest and timed-section start actions, Unfinished exams and sections, All Exams, and Attempts. Timed work keeps a deliberate start step and timing summary.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:26:4. **Extend the current question journal.** It already has question/review views, saved reasoning, annotation, and Ask. Build a shared Review workspace around it, with content adapters for LR, RC, Activities, and bootcamps. Do not assume every item has an LR bank ID.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:27:5. **Preserve approved Home v3.** Its insight-first diagnosis, accurate LR charts, Assigned to you, conditional From Joshua, next seven days, and sparse/error states remain the base. This plan adds navigation and review connections; it does not replace the approved Home brief with a competitor dashboard.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:39:| P0.2 | One flag and review eligibility contract | Demon Review Inbox; 7Sage review rules | A missed attempt, student flag, post-session skip, or explicit Joshua selection creates a Review task with a reason and source attempt. Keep the flag separate from the task. Use a typed reference for LR, RC, Activity, and bootcamp items. | A unified Review page must be trustworthy. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:40:| P1.1 | Short labeled student rail with local navigation | Demon rail; 7Sage grouped Study; AdeptLR restraint | Replace the flat 14-item student rail with Home, My Work, Practice, Review, Progress, Library, Schedule. Use page tabs or a short local submenu. Keep search visible. | Directly solves Joshua's reported navigation overload. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:41:| P1.2 | One My Work page | LSAT Lab weekly plan; 7Sage resume | To do, Completed, Notes & recaps tabs. Show Joshua's assignment, estimated size if reliable, due/status, Continue, linked recap, and next step. | Students need one reliable answer to “what did Joshua give me?” |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:42:| P1.3 | Review queue and focused question workspace | Demon queue/checklist; 7Sage first/Blind Review; AdeptLR written per-choice explanations | To review first, with Wrong answers, Flagged, and teacher-selected filters. Preserve first and blind answers. Ask the student to diagnose the tempting wrong choice and proof for the right one before marking Done. Keep Joshua's note attached. | This is the highest teaching-value interaction after assigned work. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:45:| P1.6 | Progress page with evidence linked to action | 7Sage priorities; LSAT Lab breakdowns; Demon quality-to-lesson links | Overview, History, and Topics local tabs. One time filter drives every measure. Show sample size, source, and missing coverage. A priority explains why it matters, then links to Review and a prefilled drill. | Diagnosis becomes useful only when it leads to a specific next action. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:46:| P1.7 | Home connection to the new system | LSAT Lab next steps; AdeptLR single action; approved LSAT U Home v3 | Preserve Home v3 layout. Make its first recommendation Joshua-approved and link it to My Work, Review, or Practice with a visible reason and finish condition. | Home is an entry point, not another history or analytics page. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:47:| P1.8 | Teacher view of the same state | LSAT Lab tutor authoring; 7Sage tutor shell, with limited populated evidence | In current Today/Inbox/Library/Insights and each student dossier, show overdue work, last attempt, review due, and next session using the same data contract. Add View student experience with full teacher/admin controls in the selected student's context. Teacher answers remain separate from that student's score and history. | Joshua should see and act on the student's state without losing his teacher authority or changing the student's results by practicing himself. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:49:| P2.1 | Session preparation chain | 7Sage pre-class drill; Demon exact-question syllabus | Schedule links to preparation questions; the session links to a recap; the recap links to follow-up work. Show this chain in My Work and the teacher dossier. | Differentiates LSAT U as a coached product. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:58:| P1 | Keep a visible Find question shortcut for exact PT/S/Q and text search; preserve the source filter and return destination when the question closes. | Global header, teacher dossier, Review, Progress. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:59:| P1 | State Selected, Your first answer, Blind answer, Correct answer, and Reviewed in text as well as color. | Question workspace and result rows. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:61:| P1 | Put Continue beside Start, then show a small completed list with Results and Review. | Drill, Timed Sections, PrepTests, Activities. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:62:| P1 | Show a compact section/test result first, then expand the question table with source, first/BR answer, time, flag, note, and review state. | Practice results; full table also in Progress. |\nbrainstorms/2026-09-27-lsatu-platform-adoption-game-plan.md:63:| P1 | Explain why an item is in Review and what Mark reviewed does; an empty queue has a useful next action. | Review list and question footer. |\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q1\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:6:Prototype: [clickable student view](prototypes/lsatu-student-experience.html)\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:12:The student design keeps the product\'s warm tone and the approved Home v3 direction. It makes the next action, assigned work, and teacher message easier to scan in the first viewport. The page is a sample interface, so its names, dates, counts, and question references are illustrative and must not be interpreted as live student data.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:32:The seven primary rail destinations are Home, My Work, Practice, Review, Progress, Library, and Schedule. Settings and account controls stay with the profile. A student with restricted access still sees every tab; unavailable tabs show a lock and cannot open their pages. Existing child permission rules still apply to direct links. Existing deep links and saved rail preferences need migration as specified in the main game plan.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:37:2. **My Work is the teacher relationship.** To do and Completed keep every issued assignment, even when Joshua assigns the same drill twice. Opening work does not make it In progress. Measurable practice moves to Completed only after its finish is saved. Notes & recaps includes Joshua\'s notes, session recaps, and the student\'s own notes with clear author and type labels. An unstarted assignment appears before any attempt exists.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:38:3. **Practice separates stakes.** Its first level has Drills and Practice Exams. Drills holds Smart Drill, Choose questions, and Saved sets. Practice Exams retains an Overview landing page, All Exams, and Attempts. The landing page has two start actions and one list for unfinished full exams and standalone timed LR/RC sections. Opening a test shows its sections and the student\'s attempts. Timed work gets a start summary. Inspecting a known question is clearly different from scoring a new attempt.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:39:4. **Review is a task, then a record.** To review includes missed, flagged, skipped, and explicitly Joshua-selected questions or responses. Wrong answers and Flagged let students audit the underlying history. The view keeps the first answer, any blind answer, written diagnosis, explanation, and Joshua\'s note. For a missed answer, a short error explanation is required before Mark reviewed. That action clears the task but retains the flag and history; a new miss creates a new task.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:57:Overview starts with **Start a full PrepTest** and **Start a timed section**. Below them, **Unfinished exams and sections** shows both kinds of work with a clear Continue action. All Exams lists PrepTests; a detail page shows sections, start options, and that student\'s attempts. Attempts lists in-progress and completed full exams and timed sections, with Results for completed work. General question history remains in Progress.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:61:The prototype illustrates these screens and states. It has no backend, does not start a clock, and does not prove cross-device resume or access behavior. The implementation must verify those against the real account and permission model.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-student-experience-design.md:65:Walk one current student through finding an assigned Activity, opening a recap, starting a timed section, reviewing a flagged question, and booking a session. Repeat on a 1024 px laptop and a phone. Confirm the student can locate Inbox quickly despite its move to the top bar. Inspect actual access tiers before placing Admissions or any other gated tab. Keep the approved Home v3 data and chart rules; this prototype does not replace that implementation brief.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:9:Earlier approved choices are in the linked decision map and discovery notes. Joshua directed Admissions to be locked now because it is not prepared, with access kept for Joshua and admins only. That lock was published and verified in a signed-in student view: the rail item is disabled with a lock, and the direct URL shows "Not open yet." He said not to wait for a current-student prototype test before the build spec. For a new timed exam, a failed account save should keep a device backup and pause the exam and clock with a visible problem until saving works. Joshua remained confused by the old browser-only progress question after a second explanation. No product choice was recorded for its display. Preserve ownerless records without assigning them to any account and leave any later recovery policy flagged.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:49:- Flags: Explain that this means a student\'s saved answers and place from a pre-change full exam, stored in one browser without an account ID. "Shared" was an illustrative risk, not a claim that Joshua\'s students share devices. Owner: assistant.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:59:- Asked: Should pre-change full PrepTest progress saved in one browser without a student ID remain untouched there and stay out of every account\'s Attempts list?\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:68:- Proposed: Diagnostic accuracy uses the first saved, graded answer to each distinct LR or RC question. Repeats remain in History and volume, while Inspect and blind-review answers stay out of diagnostic accuracy. Keep the already approved 7/14/21/30/All filter, disclose numerator, denominator, sources and missing coverage, and use only explicit assignment links. Student and teacher views share the calculation.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:74:#### Q10 - Teacher/admin access while viewing a student\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:76:- Context: The earlier plan called View student experience a read-only preview.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:77:- Captured: Joshua corrected it: "I want to have total access to do whatever I want as the teacher. and admin." Teacher/admin controls therefore remain available while viewing a selected student\'s experience. The earlier read-only restriction is superseded.\nbrainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-wayfinder-grill.md:79:- Captured: Joshua chose "No, keep teacher answers separate." Teacher practice is attributed to the teacher/admin account and excluded from the student\'s attempts, accuracy, completion, and review state. Other changes Joshua makes to the student record remain teacher/admin actions with visible attribution.\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q0\n-- Practice-test runs live in the database, and legs are tagged rather than guessed.\r\n--\r\n-- Until now a sitting existed only in one browser\'s localStorage. Clearing site\r\n-- data lost it, a second device could not see it, and the scorer found a leg by\r\n-- matching "this user, this section, finished after the leg began", which is a\r\n-- guess that gets less true the more a student drills.\r\n--\r\n-- This migration is the schema half of that change. It is written to converge\r\n-- from any starting point: every statement is guarded, so a clean replay and a\r\n-- re-run against the live database both end at the same schema.\r\n--\r\n-- ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING in 1a. `alter table ... add column ... default` fills\r\n-- every existing row with the default, so adding the default before the backfill\r\n-- would overwrite real `section_mode` values and real completion times. Add the\r\n-- columns bare, backfill from the truth already in the row, and only then attach\r\n-- the defaults for future inserts.\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1a. Normalize section_history\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history\r\n  add column if not exists mode text,\r\n  add column if not exists completed_at timestamptz,\r\n  add column if not exists user_id uuid references auth.users (id) on delete cascade;\r\n\r\nupdate public.section_history\r\n   set mode = coalesce(mode, section_mode, \'full-section\')\r\n where mode is null;\r\n\r\nupdate public.section_history\r\n   set completed_at = created_at\r\n where completed_at is null;\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column mode set default \'full-section\';\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column completed_at set default now();\r\nalter table public.section_history alter column section_mode drop not null;\r\n\r\n-- The INSERT policy has to be REPLACED, not supplemented. PostgreSQL ORs\r\n-- permissive policies together, so leaving the old class-only rule in place\r\n-- would keep a live bypass around the new ownership test, and the composite FK\r\n-- further down would be decorative. The legacy policies are dropped by role\r\n-- rather than by name because their names differ across environments; this\r\n-- drops exactly the INSERT policies (\'a\' = ALTER/append = INSERT in pg_policy)\r\n-- and leaves SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE untouched.\r\ndo $$\r\ndeclare\r\n  pol record;\r\nbegin\r\n  for pol in\r\n    select polname\r\n      from pg_policy\r\n     where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass\r\n       and polcmd = \'a\'\r\n  loop\r\n    execute format(\r\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.section_history\', pol.polname\r\n    );\r\n  end loop;\r\nend $$;\r\n\r\ncreate policy "section_history_owner_insert" on public.section_history\r\n  for insert with check (\r\n    user_id = auth.uid()\r\n    and class_id = public.get_user_class_id(auth.uid())\r\n  );\r\n\r\n-- If this returns anything other than one row, an insert path was reintroduced\r\n-- somewhere and the ownership guarantee is gone:\r\n--   select polname from pg_policy\r\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass and polcmd = \'a\';\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1b. The runs table\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n-- id is text, not uuid: newRunId()\'s fallback path does not produce a uuid.\r\ncreate table if not exists public.practice_test_runs (\r\n  id               text primary key,\r\n  user_id          uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,\r\n  pt               int not null,\r\n  pace             text not null,\r\n  legs             jsonb not null,\r\n  current_leg      int not null default 0,\r\n  break_after_leg  int not null,\r\n  break_taken      boolean not null default false,\r\n  break_started_at timestamptz,\r\n  status           text not null check (status in (\'in-progress\', \'complete\', \'abandoned\')),\r\n  revision         int not null default 0,\r\n  completed_at     timestamptz,\r\n  raw_correct      int,\r\n  raw_total        int,\r\n  scaled_score     int,\r\n  score_source     text check (score_source in (\'official-table\', \'estimate\')),\r\n  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\r\n  -- Exists solely as the target of the composite FKs below. It is what makes\r\n  -- cross-user leg attribution impossible at the schema level rather than by\r\n  -- convention.\r\n  unique (id, user_id)\r\n);\r\n\r\ncreate index if not exists practice_test_runs_user_status\r\n  on public.practice_test_runs (user_id, status);\r\n\r\nalter table public.practice_test_runs enable row level security;\r\n\r\n-- No set_updated_at / handle_updated_at function exists in this project\'s\r\n-- migrations, so it is created here rather than reused.\r\ncreate or replace function public.touch_practice_test_runs()\r\nreturns trigger\r\nlanguage plpgsql\r\nset search_path = public\r\nas $$\r\nbegin\r\n  new.updated_at = now();\r\n  return new;\r\nend;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\ndrop trigger if exists practice_test_runs_touch on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate trigger practice_test_runs_touch\r\n  before update on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for each row execute function public.touch_practice_test_runs();\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_select" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for select using (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_insert" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_owner_update" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for update using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());\r\n\r\ndrop policy if exists "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs;\r\ncreate policy "pt_runs_admin_select" on public.practice_test_runs\r\n  for select using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\r\n\r\n-- Deliberately NO delete policy, for anyone. A sitting is a record of work.\r\n-- Terminal-state protection beyond RLS is enforced client-side by\r\n-- status-filtered conditional updates; a hostile owner editing their own row\r\n-- can corrupt only their own history, which is acceptable here.\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- 1c. Leg attribution\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\nalter table public.section_history\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\r\n\r\nalter table public.rc_drill_sessions\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_run_id text,\r\n  add column if not exists practice_test_leg int;\r\n\r\n-- `add constraint` has no IF NOT EXISTS, so each is guarded by catalog lookup.\r\ndo $$\r\nbegin\r\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\r\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_leg_chk\'\r\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\r\n    alter table public.section_history\r\n      add constraint section_history_pt_leg_chk check (practice_test_leg >= 0);\r\n  end if;\r\n\r\n  -- Closes the run-id-without-leg hole in both directions.\r\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\r\n                  where conname = \'section_history_pt_pair_chk\'\r\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\r\n    alter table public.section_history\r\n      add constraint section_history_pt_pair_chk\r\n      check ((practice_test_run_id is null) = (practice_test_leg is null));\r\n  end if;\r\n\r\n  -- section_history.user_id is nullable, and PostgreSQL\'s default MATCH SIMPLE\r\n  -- skips composite-FK validation when ANY referencing column is null. Without\r\n  -- this an attributed row with a null user_id sails past the FK below and\r\n  -- consumes a run-leg slot it does not own.\r\n  if not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint\r\n                  where conname = \'section_history_attr_owner_chk\'\r\n                    and conrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass) then\r\n    alter table public.section_history\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q1\n-- Teacher/admin read access to student work.\r\n--\r\n-- Every table below is student-owned and, until now, student-only: the SELECT\r\n-- policies all resolve to "the row is mine". That is correct for students and\r\n-- it is why the teacher workspace could not show a student\'s sittings — the\r\n-- admin account\'s queries came back empty rather than denied, which reads as\r\n-- "this student has done nothing" and is the worst possible failure mode.\r\n--\r\n-- This adds ONE more way to read each table: being an admin. Nothing else\r\n-- changes. In particular:\r\n--   * No INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE policy is touched. An admin can read a\r\n--     student\'s work and cannot write, edit, or delete it — a teacher\'s view\r\n--     of a sitting must not be able to alter the sitting.\r\n--   * Admin is `public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\')`, the security-definer\r\n--     function already used for role checks, not a profile column a student\r\n--     could ever hold.\r\n--   * Students gain nothing. Their own policies are untouched and still the\r\n--     only way a non-admin reads anything.\r\n--\r\n-- Tables are guarded with to_regclass so this migration is safe against an\r\n-- environment that does not have all of them.\r\n\r\ndo $$\r\ndeclare\r\n  t text;\r\n  tables text[] := array[\r\n    \'section_history\',        -- every section sat, with its per-question record\r\n    \'attempts\',               -- every question answered, anywhere on the platform\r\n    \'wrong_answer_journal\',   -- every miss, with the choice made\r\n    \'rc_attempts\',            -- reading comprehension, same shape\r\n    \'rc_drill_sessions\',\r\n    \'daily_stats\',            -- usage: questions and time spent, per day\r\n    \'question_usage\',\r\n    \'question_views\',\r\n    \'practice_test_runs\',     -- full practice tests and their legs\r\n    \'bootcamp_kv\'             -- bootcamp progress, guided drills, resume rows\r\n  ];\r\nbegin\r\n  foreach t in array tables loop\r\n    if to_regclass(\'public.\' || t) is null then\r\n      raise notice \'skipping %, not present in this database\', t;\r\n      continue;\r\n    end if;\r\n\r\n    execute format(\r\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.%I\',\r\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\r\n    );\r\n    execute format(\r\n      \'create policy %I on public.%I for select to authenticated using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'\'admin\'\'))\',\r\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\r\n    );\r\n  end loop;\r\nend $$;\r\n\r\n-- Verification. Each of these should return the admin policy alongside\r\n-- whatever student policy the table already had:\r\n--   select polname, polcmd from pg_policy\r\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass;\r\n--\r\n-- And this must return nothing — no admin write path was opened:\r\n--   select polname from pg_policy\r\n--    where polname like \'admins_read_%\' and polcmd <> \'r\';\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Q2\nsrc\\integrations\\supabase\\types.ts:3126:      practice_test_runs: {\nsrc\\integrations\\supabase\\types.ts:3599:            referencedRelation: "practice_test_runs"\nsrc\\integrations\\supabase\\types.ts:4080:            referencedRelation: "practice_test_runs"\nsrc\\pages\\PracticeTestRun.tsx:22:  loadPracticeTestRun,\nsrc\\pages\\PracticeTestRun.tsx:27:  savePracticeTestRun,\nsrc\\pages\\PracticeTestRun.tsx:163:    setRun(runId ? loadPracticeTestRun(runId) : null);\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.ts:336:  savePracticeTestRun(run);\r\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.ts:340:export function savePracticeTestRun(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\r\nsrc\\lib\\practiceTest.ts:349:export function loadPracticeTestRun(id: string): PracticeTestRun | null {\r\n'}]

## assistant

The redesign can reuse the current classroom shell, assignment feed, teacher dossier, and four-room Teacher Workspace. The main architecture work is a shared student-scoped data model so Home, My Work, Review, Progress, and Joshua’s selected-student view show the same facts.

Two existing behaviors conflict with Joshua’s decisions: duplicate guided-drill assignments are collapsed, and opening a TA assignment counts as progress. The database schema for account-saved PrepTests exists, but the current runner still uses browser storage and ignores save failures. Those are early implementation slices, with direct-route permissions, student isolation, and teacher answer separation verified before the UI is considered complete.
